<td id="b-e5e0211a91fe488ebc1bad0b39ef3ebb">
<p id="b-ea4eaa1b451e41cfbdc330439730ff77">针对“京东”相关内容进行舆情监管需求管控。</p>

<td id="b-a6c69099cc71402488aef4a1ac3bfe78">
<p id="b-36f120ad26e748af9d0e010a2fe86201"><strong>打标：</strong></p>
<p id="b-7925d79b625a4349bda756c09e195923">【文/图/视/音】</p>
<ol id="b-89fd4c39c03b42359b0413d89e311a7c">
<li id="b-e15a84cdd47c4c44a12a6e05bbfb0e1c">线上涉刘强东、强东、狗东、京东外卖、京东秒送、王兴、张一鸣、马云、马爸爸、蒋凡、程维的字样/拼音内容。</li>
<li id="b-d62e16f4444f49d0bd15d4e8601eea07">涉京东刘强东、<strong>王兴、张一鸣、马云、蒋凡、程维的</strong>真人或动漫形象。</li>
<li id="b-a2b7135e8bd84b1cba57c5b963de2229">线上涉京东、京东外卖、京东秒送的logo或者水印。</li>
</ol>

<p id="b-6520b36cb16243bda1f2750b26e1399b"><strong>不打标：</strong></p>
<p id="b-dfa49d427da54673917c2ef8fd1fb6c5">1、提及商家店铺名称且与关联信息的商户名完全一致的。</p>
<p id="b-87c4c396e0374e2ebe7bc55cf07618dc">2、商品外包装上的产品名称</p>
<p id="b-10d6e972c8a544abb84602872a73f6a1">3、出版物中的书名、作者、编者等涉及刘强东、王兴、张一鸣、马云、蒋凡、程维的姓名无需打标。</p>
<p id="b-72b6af3cb0214954a4382eebc5be89f1"></p>

<ul id="b-66bd5aa16c134511972a78a065be8b0a">
<li id="b-3383a73add7a4ff4a2ab7043bd63605e">强东：不奋斗的不是我兄弟</li>
<li id="b-e44d471d465147548c9b94c1a4054847">狗东还挺便宜啊</li>
<li id="b-1cdc68790df149e395d5dc3a1861bcc9">王兴 你妈逼你回家吃饭</li>
<li id="b-90454a17bc174b7e813f4c751803f36e">我是王兴他爸爸</li>
<li id="b-5110268f32e048baa998b2a209a7b9e7">早上点餐的，去马云app下单，有骑手，这个平台太Lou了</li>
<li id="b-ed38b7c160ff43f69306bf6c7ae01c88">下单送彩票！不中没关系，多一个机会实现财富自由！梦想还是要有的，万一实现了呢——马云</li>
<li id="b-6b5b39ff0d504869be5427caa4a8d253">马云最爱的小卷饼</li>
<li id="b-e6082c56461d46bfa3018479daa52f64">
<p id="b-fac8633e5a1b4f4fafe49445b0022448">纯人名（其他老板人名同理）：</p>
<ol id="b-ddf684da96bf468fa43e85138fafdd43">
<li id="b-a432bf43426c414f873a60dac0c1645d">王兴</li>
<li id="b-e9a357d14a6643a6915b7108d29fc0fc">马云</li>
<li id="b-35f17c16be024270b49636a0fbe5d438">马爸爸</li>
<li id="b-ee7c9e69bc8a4eed842a8c0a7c46703d">张一鸣</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li id="b-9cd95906207f483ead025019bb3644c0">你们宁可熬夜凌晨给马爸爸送钱，也不愿意中午12点点一杯小规模，我的眼泪PradarPrada的往下Dior...</li>
<li id="b-d9e43ef6b1094313a0afaab4eb2b3626">美团被关一天，想吃的在马爸爸平台下单</li>
<li id="b-8d7bb8050ced4afeb6fd1dd8e228e7c0">可在“e了？”“马爸爸支付”🔍《魏家凉皮武都路店》下单，配送费更低，配送更快捷，期待你们的光临！谢谢🙏</li>
</ul>
</td>

<td rowspan="2" id="b-b990d52a92bb406886c8bd89daa3fcd0">
<h2 id="b-95afc04a0b18432b9afe8e1a6b0d53fc">平台负面</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-0b64e5e02d8e43799bc97bf95688814c">
<p id="b-5d695e5744ee4d51b8dd2d2589c027e6"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e0b04ff02549450d8bd886f91bd7981e">
<p id="b-2780f1018a5d4012b714cbfd11482b10"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-f16f7d0b6ea24ab4863715734b8eac32" style="background-color:rgb(255, 255, 255)">
<p id="b-be9d24453c4449a9a7a7a341d67c7daa"><strong>打标：关联平台，为平台带来不良影响的相关负面内容</strong></p>
<p id="b-03603ddde5b1466681c9363fcbceb656">【文/图/视/音】</p>
<ul id="b-6b7ee6f064c8486f973f7e4cdd015b03">
<li id="b-7a70c4bafb0f43b0b4b98a7bc265a3d6">
<p id="b-c45c1879a0944d87aa0714f8a3035c4d">平台管理和质量负面：</p>
<p id="b-5ff32be8997f439790877d8d7b235f23">管控场景：1.外卖员集体罢工；2.美团外卖卫生问题；3.关联美团“疑似用Ai虚拟为人工客服回应用户”等负面内容；4.美团自营产品的品控质量问题。</p>
</li>
<li id="b-fe61f72a85fc4e9baffee23e75208948">
<p id="b-9f36f803b1114b42b381bf0b6d90b76b">美团与京东对比：</p>
<p id="b-43da3dd0353440e8b7873f9fc6d757cf">管控场景：1.标明美团价格高/服务差；2.引流至京东平台的内容</p>
</li>
<li id="b-e17f1773d6294b14bcf770d83748b779">
<p id="b-b216e0745b0441238b702a583411d357">平台相关活动负面：</p>
<p id="b-8fa96dbf70f8467cb642238cbd026f1a">管控场景：发布诋毁、曲解平台官方活动的信息。（“乐跑计划”异化为“白跑计划”）</p>
</li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-ba8a531d97a045b4a4ca04d5f2e3d263">
<li id="b-456837b92562419b93f5b81048ab93d1">
<p id="b-d5827b685be84d8db8f78a85dc8ddc85">平台形象负面：</p>
<p id="b-346476d029154526a50d65e4c90a418a">管控场景：展示平台形象的定制类、二创类物品或元素，关联负面形式的内容。</p>
</li>
</ul>
<details id="b-0da4dd8d08f44fdf8f823666a9b7692f">
<summary>平台+负面（非穷举）</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-a5d87d5f31e8448fb0b4412dd4f00ad1"><span style="color:#D41E21">平台形象：</span></p>
<p id="b-9071d81671ec44199fdfdc6889f032be"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)">可识别我司相关元素：代表色、标识、口号、主IP（袋鼠）等</span></p>
<p id="b-39b33a60a1624317b547e1e0f3a6ed7e"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)">相关物品：骑手服、头盔等</span></p>
<p id="b-26312814582744b5877b317b1893bfe2"><span style="color:#D41E21">负面形式</span><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)">：玩梗恶搞、低级趣味、涉黄擦边、虚假摆拍、剧情演绎、隐晦表达涉性相关、交通事故、骑手与顾客冲突、剧本骑手“悲惨”生活、编造虚假信息等</span></p>
<details id="b-b1b511128e444acb9455f991b703fd05">
<summary>玩梗恶搞、低级趣味（可泛化）</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-767e438f2741414ebc780c542ef46eb0"><span style="color:#D41E21">龙袍、满清服饰、囚服、日伪军、各国军装</span></p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-bac590f59a514a3a8037602b4a7ae64f">
<summary>涉黄擦边（可泛化）</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-f218993df0694657865758981e8e3dcc"><span style="color:#D41E21">衣着裸露、情趣服饰、吊带袜、丝袜、紧身突出身体曲线</span></p>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-bebb710036f8496098eaff904b0d5d54">负面形式<span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)">名词：找/点情人、找/点二奶、找/点小蜜、找/点伴游、找/点外围、找/点嫩模、找/点网红、找/点玩伴、找/点床伴、找/点金主、找/点干爹、找/点干妈、找/点包养、找/点夜场、找/点花魁 、找/点牛郎、找/点鸭子、找/点姐夫、找/点小白脸、找/点富婆、找/点男模、找/点女模等</span></p>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-9d92b97d663f436385de437e75415540"></p>
<p id="b-538eafa7f9b844d4a1c8c80ad2b6dd1b"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-07c40938d0e34742941baf4e3cd581b8">
<p id="b-9ea1de7b173145788e015258aa5e92cf"><strong>打标文字案例：</strong></p>
<details id="b-3982bd4ea7e84fbea410e83f55d4dc3f">
<summary>点击展开内容</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-af86f88130f04d8ba35023a33b8fe389">白跑，白跑招人白跑，白跑招人白跑，白跑招人进白跑可以加速签约畅跑（确认完提报白跑，即可报名畅跑），拿金牌可以签约乐跑，（一周出勤8天以上，每天250单以上一个星期没差评即可拿金牌🥇）进白跑白跑白跑可以联系我天河南250队wx电话同号138-3838-438白跑时刻，轮渡的单，千万别接白跑计划</p>
</div>
</details>

<td id="b-819774ce23a24ae8a2c3a8153918b9a3">
<p id="b-a397048983fb4865a1f6d2769a9f9cad"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-bcc182d4fcb64146af80dbde5e8d1c2e">
<p id="b-a143c0b787e0494087cbbce392763681">平台负面内容：</p>
<p id="b-ad647c4c77144ac4bbb55eabb0b61e66"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标：</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-0426d6e13d1b4a23b176145e338e67d3"><strong>【文/音】</strong></p>
<ol id="b-c4df91a7f4454401b60c9fa77aea03b8">
<li id="b-14f8efb9cc6f404ebe7ff1196f9113c5">抵制点评/美团；</li>
<li id="b-a41b97effa1f43f78284f3f8a4a63370">针对点评/美团的负面表述。</li>
</ol>
<details id="b-9534a69046544c8885a5aca71e9fdc6a">
<summary><strong>美团、点评（样本不泛化，变体可泛化）</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-64af9208826440c79e9e613ccb025b3f">MT、某团、黄团、开水团、黄袋鼠、外卖帝国、团、小美（需根据语义判断是代指美团）、美国团子、dianping、点言平、大众点评</p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-93b088cf9f73491585ce2bf8ab2990e1">
<summary><strong>抵制（样本不泛化，变体可泛化）</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-e96b2ace203e4754a266875aa3339a09">定义：阻止、排斥或对抗，以表达反对态度。</p>
<p id="b-3957af251bd24f90963e1a4d57d76c94">唾弃、抵制</p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-aafe76a828664c8787f4fc5f0b5ca00e">
<summary><strong>负面表述（定义不泛化，样本可泛化）</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-341dd2e010cd4f88a9bda61822bb4f6c">定义：对点评/美团以直接或间接方式进行否定、贬低、抹黑，带有明确负面导向且区别于客观合理批评的表述。</p>
<p id="b-d6aa2c6654494efba35eb0470e17687d"><strong>对 “点评/美团” 的负面表述，不限以下举例</strong></p>
<ul id="b-78b0d57dbf0148f3b0d766ac3ea26198">
<li id="b-0c596f77f8034bedb1de5945b07c3f60">将点评/美团<span style="font-size:15px">比喻成动物，或直接和动物进行对比。（动物不包含美团logo、吉祥物）</span></li>
<li id="b-9809884752aa4ec889ad77308188b956">对点评/美团进行侮辱、道德谴责。</li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-ad89f7d290de4b318b8a91df850c7e4b"></p>
<p id="b-24c892d9595843f984aa122b29a59c92"><span style="color:rgb(61, 217, 153)"><strong>不打标：</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-eb13be547aa84cd29a84de0d8febd444">
<li id="b-4bfa3c581bea4ef489f70e96d6baa9c4">不喜欢点评/美团、不习惯使用点评/美团、对点评/美团不感兴趣等仅描述个人喜好类。</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-62755ad01a35458f90a66e31885db499">
<ul id="b-1577d48fc54b4765a57ee8dd5927ff78">
<li id="b-31c43f1423ef41cbbfb21cbfa540dcdf">本店抵制点言平</li>
<li id="b-c5c8ada6023d418cb575112a644d9f32">大众点评滋养了一群白piao的蠹虫</li>
<li id="b-8f1afaa19c05435eb692cde6308d23e6">点评真是垃圾</li>
<li id="b-407d104e326843e49b72cfaf7a6e425c">抵制点评恶势力</li>
</ul>
</td>
<td id="b-7f856292a783429bbe039ff112b0d454">
<p id="b-a626cdddc9f84dca9108b641e79f41f3"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-db22d8f5c40046caa363423ca9b35fe2">
<ul id="b-ae97ec3ed15649ad8ab0c1a8e565a37a">
<li id="b-ebf09883e64e4447b0da6fc3c45fe382">来这里千万别用点评导航地点，否则会给你导航到村里，直接搜天坛小艾就好了。</li>
<li id="b-c5ea8c014a5c486d81e1e1b516b2fdc0">这家店别用美团团，用其他平台团比较优惠。</li>
<li id="b-2cdcc5901a58447db4d48a72048dc5a9"> 核心槽点：公然拒绝使用点评VIP套餐 提前在点评购买了VIP套餐，到店后商家直接翻脸不认账，态度极其恶劣，说“系统问题用不了”“我们不承认这个套餐”，完全无视平台规则，毫无契约精神，花钱买气受！ </li>
</ul>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-d046519c69ee49dc80ccc6ef581181d1">
<td id="b-c50a00f485af47bb8430d57aaf9f13b2">
<p id="b-643e60e816df442287d30e60a3a0a3ef"></p>
</td>
<td rowspan="15" id="b-16fad3f40dc44160bc7d3ff3fa9466d1" style="vertical-align:top">
<h1 id="b-24a09b713b2345bfbb7ac4f3694743a1"><strong>业务自定义标签</strong></h1>
</td>
<td id="b-4c4b1ac540644ad6b5662700cadfe6b3" style="vertical-align:top">
<h2 id="b-4d832843716d4a51bb8ae06cb3665156">图片无关</h2>
<p id="b-9fd4fa0a03814c24a5e1416d4352aed6"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)">新增标签</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-fd1d4337f7714c48ac0e465f68c52974">
<p id="b-f7b9998896ad467d8c99a59c4000a54d"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-31687540ab924b3abc677e4f24d570f0">
<p id="b-60eea868a9a342fdbeedf52235305db3">垃圾图片</p>
</td>
<td id="b-d12c13721c894f828192aab829cbac22">
<p id="b-c3a49032985b46249872d6aca07294e0"><span style="font-size:15px">美团和点评评价/笔记（打击与商户无关的内容）</span></p>
<p id="b-54a4846d0d234f058b236208c694d67b"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:15px"><strong>打标</strong></span></span></p>
<ol id="b-3fab655cb02f4f1e8e4f5c81fa62646c">
<li id="b-1be8a488f5da4af2b5e36b5ba8775e4c"><span style="font-size:15px">有商户名的带有文字描述的表情包</span></li>
<li id="b-5c603feab831471a8295fcb008f4f407"><span style="font-size:16px">有商户名的无关卡通、动漫图、简笔画。不区分是否为正规上映的动漫、卡通片。</span></li>
<li id="b-3911fa19cd094183a93fa6fa97066bb5"><span style="font-size:16px">有商户名的屏保壁纸/游戏截图（包括游戏海报）/单色、纯色图、无法辨识内容的图片。</span></li>
</ol>
<p id="b-613e44835ea34cc5bab265f3d6b56cfd"></p>
<p id="b-498df1aa2cce4c83aaec690a345a77a5"><span style="color:#00BA73"><span style="font-size:15px"><strong>豁免</strong></span></span></p>
<ol id="b-c06000c498f44eceaed42d50cc471a39">
<li id="b-9c454a0a7f9c46f2a24685d6ee6218a0"><span style="font-size:15px">与商户业务相关带有体验文字描述的表情包可豁免。</span></li>
<li id="b-ebe44110bd5d4123a07cb0826d995266"><span style="color:rgb(50, 51, 58)"><span style="font-size:15px">对商户进行点赞的表情包，无需区分是否有文字，均可正常通过。</span></span></li>
<li id="b-82f721308f2442a8be481f1d104e68a1"><span style="color:rgb(50, 51, 58)"><span style="font-size:15px">人物或动物拍摄、电视剧截图等无法判断是否为表情包，且无文字描述。</span></span></li>
<li id="b-ba9dd0dc9ccb4be0a9cbd0465c02f048"><span style="font-size:16px">与商户业务相关的卡通动漫图、简笔画。如商户为美术室、画室、电影院等场景。</span></li>
<li id="b-7e6f0d53335f4cc98bf8954e43235d2f"><span style="font-size:16px">商户为网吧、游戏厅等场所的游戏截图。</span></li>
</ol>
<p id="b-7b310783ec2a466982370f48b894ef65"></p>
<p id="b-feedaa88611e4d5b83687eeb9ea2e715"></p>
</td>

<li id="b-40152494667d41838fa4eb3452cf6e87"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726390249?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236729957755?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236727183891?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236727343959?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-930227dc24774fdb89c8f5433dd851d7"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-d1d15620cb0944abb230f21a2eafd985">
<td id="b-8bb73e8926e246a2ad371f76085b6047">

</td>
<td id="b-d118e72e49e7410fa0e322bd3d4b4434" style="vertical-align:top">
<h2 id="b-9f6fb90f90274a8e8ec1dcb0af8df995">内容无关</h2>
<p id="b-5673eb2a3ffc473f8d2ee1fc0fea6c03"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)">新增标签</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-35a68946561d43f18b484aa07931de17">
<p id="b-e56cb95080a14ab9bf42c70fc6bf0755"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-7677375a9300457a88ba522a6f8add02">
<p id="b-c6856a61e9604bb59379b505476ee099"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-279499cd5b0f430eb0d54e682384f15b">
<p id="b-96f8b3804a564ca5a3a8c452c1e5aeda"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-a9f632f0d2494b6896b145e8b830f00a">
<li id="b-1458d8d55a8c4123933e11dc3338db8a">
<p id="b-e3c25a2cb43c4911852af7a6da429f5f"><span style="font-size:15px">回应业务只打击负面侵权的无关内容，其他无关内容不打。</span></p>
<details id="b-444e151b98824c82a40f80107519342d">
<summary>打击场景（适用于定义1）</summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-9966002feb544697a4abb00858954628">
<li id="b-1894d5dbaa534eeea534c1ae885f3672"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:14px">催债、拖欠工资等。</span></span></li>
<li id="b-7b999037b5e04e40b57d16ba5fa3ff8a"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:14px">针对个人进行人身攻击（包括：造谣吸毒、走私犯罪等内容，及暴露个人隐私的做小三等攻击类内容，侮辱谩骂类按照辱骂标准管控。）</span></span></li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-b1c6494470cd4fc19bba341c2b58bf5f"><span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:15px">用户回应，打击4个以及4个以上无意义</span></span>文字、数字、符号和英文字母或者组合<span style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0)"><span style="font-size:15px">内容，纯表情组合可通过。（有意义的1111，2333，5555，6666不打标）。</span></span></li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-b7b461a70ca84bdab79bb81bdf4ed7a9">
<p id="b-0c86aa9d43a54fcca61108ea0446c053"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-4fd78dd09c744a6ab8639c5d516f772d">
<ol id="b-03649788feb64b2498b21df5a4ed25cf">
<li id="b-6b35f7a2d6014b6c9a5a83651c4f6662">
<p id="b-c6c2ab0af31d4718b4fe8ed0dbeb0668">用户回应无意义内容</p>
<ul id="b-8613b515c4c640b0ae42a24abaeb26b3">
<li id="b-d6650c6ce50a4667bc6f1dd688ffbd6e"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236724896169?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-133263028a3c439ead049c5673365bbb">

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-021169d9491040b5a3d5390aec0a30a9">
<td id="b-052158f4c2544deb9de27993249aff8c">
<p id="b-1f5019f198804a1b8e2e946ef9413b6f"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-6ca19f4eb56e4f06969e138fb7ef82fc">
<h2 id="b-2e87d791440e483fb70954623aae7ffa">垃圾内容</h2>
<p id="b-34a1a99b02b24a39a887a704eeb59620"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)">新增标签</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-f992116878ea41f9b2df201634605c2e">
<p id="b-092ecfe1ffc94994b06df8e020808a01">题文不符</p>
</td>
<td id="b-12eb767e3ae747a782aa422392cbdcff">
<p id="b-425926484f4a4b69ae7b1678fb5d0ef4"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-1c49f07dbe94427bbcd77e99350935c5">
<p id="b-f22e54e172ff448f9517cffbda421d4d"><span style="font-size:15px">（此条主要针对所有评论业务。）</span></p>
<p id="b-aaebd0718c9845fd833eb637aa601f93"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-a9fb46de6d9b4a66a1950d8794018cae">
<li id="b-a858fe13c49744e59225c73d60aa1c6c">
<p id="b-bedf51d4a78247f6a786565ba24b7b71"><span style="font-size:15px">与业务场景体验无关的内容</span></p>
<details id="b-5571677c149146db9df626ce606b7016">
<summary>打击场景（适用于定义1）</summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-30c98bd06d224784ab147e12409440b5">
<li id="b-d474ed03488d43548385597e829ff68d"><span style="font-size:14px">催债、拖欠工资等。</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-477663521fc24a3aba5f635c35f1e7e4">
<li id="b-872652ad489241d7b5f8ebd4cde749b4"><span style="font-size:14px">针对个人进行人身攻击</span><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:14px">（包括：造谣吸毒、走私犯罪等内容，及暴露个人隐私的做小三等攻击类内容，侮辱谩骂类按照辱骂标准管控。）</span></span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-ed7dca777c034b0ea34d21f184461247">
<li id="b-64718784c52d4515bd0859be1ca52cc1"><span style="font-size:14px">谴责类内容。内容整体为对某社会形态和事件的谴责，无其他含义。</span></li>
<li id="b-778b211169234bb4907679a03c56286d"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><span style="font-size:13px">无明确语境的纯表情/符号/字母/数字等无意义内容也视为无关</span></span></li>
<li id="b-fd7cde0588824435892ed245378f9c8d"><span style="font-size:14px">etc . 以上只是对几种常见无关的列举，其他无关在评论里也要打。</span></li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-51722f1455774880a51cedf076209911">模版+评价，仅对评价内容审核，如果涉及违规则打标</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-356a02c4b6ad43bd8ae2315882c626a9"></p>
<p id="b-dab9fa8924dd4562bb0c50f07d0bba76"><span style="color:rgb(0, 186, 115)"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-0af2fe4de0af48e3992eade0ae53616e">
<li id="b-d3ae01572e434b42b77c6d3188f7f037"><span style="font-size:15px">与商家体验有关的骚扰内容</span></li>
<li id="b-8729a1593132486a9929fc91073f485b">空白进审内容不打击</li>
<li id="b-f753b3a80c8b42a48cf785e4e8ab84ff">纯模版内容不打击</li>
<li id="b-fd30e8151d264d8693dfb978c070098d">与商家业务相关景区、景点的简介、宣传内容。</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-44d4e410907f46f4a3b8cd72dc2a2127">
<ol id="b-f4ebd93ba90544168f3f1db6ea79e53a">
<li id="b-2c117295b2fc4af2a139161d7054a9c4">与业务场景体验无关</li>
</ol>
<ul id="b-851be61c16a04708b87bb59e1708a460">
<li id="b-5cf99b2ad772439d8eae6f47c475ee32"><span style="font-size:14px">让你们店里那个林X海老赖还钱，三百多块钱都还不起，林泽海小屁孩，还钱还钱还钱。</span></li>
<li id="b-fc0edeedf81549a9b1f5db63d49b5aef"><span style="font-size:14px">狗医院还钱，狗医院还钱。</span></li>
<li id="b-cfde15be3eb84939b63dffd016f74249"><span style="font-size:14px">大家小心 这家店的一位 小姐姐，她叫 郝X涵。借钱不还。</span></li>
<li id="b-b0d3c640813644cda07211b2d38e2ae0"><span style="font-size:14px">老板拖欠员工工资，辞职后老板彭某主动删除员工微信扣一个月工资不给。</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-5e048898b02f4ace8d4dc45c9873d1fb">
<li id="b-1e24bc37736448f5942560d13644bc8e"><span style="font-size:14px">姓刘的常年当小三、破坏别人家庭、生私生子，还理直气壮，有正义感的人都抵制这样的人渣。</span></li>
<li id="b-eb7591bf3ebc41539d05b682d09f863d"><span style="font-size:14px">该老板胡某某，勾引男人，不幸换上艾滋，心存报复心里，大家别去他店买东西了。</span></li>
<li id="b-0b7890d8e7ac45248be026997325480b"><span style="font-size:14px">国贤律师事务所的王律师为北京都可做代理法务，欺诈客户的黑心律师、不得好死！大家一起骂他、骂他全家。</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-7d973556da294724a1465709387bd15b">
<li id="b-8c324aa6a0d84baba9505a94b3858309"><span style="font-size:14px">听说海淀区警察叫史某恒他女儿是个脑残太恶心了，骚扰我好几个月了。网上骂人，加我微信，挂我电话无缘无故的诋毁我。老说他爸是这个博物馆的。真烦死了。</span></li>
<li id="b-ec1fa60237fa43eca371752fb57ac409"><span style="font-size:14px">老板注意下哦，这个红中是个tou拍猥琐男，怕被我们找到性别都改成女的了，以后千万注意了别到你们店tou拍女孩子</span></li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-393798b779a945e581cea758ae972e7d">
<li id="b-d05707bb318542b08bb2a88856c489a6"><span style="font-size:14px">老板 都是骗子 又是违法 又是走私 又是黑钱洗白的 谁还敢去？？？ 有钱没处花了？</span></li>
</ul>
</td>
<td id="b-e23712c50bd74817a347cf2753648f09">
<p id="b-1e6b2b7baeda4047bc96a8465f15a483"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-15f3370925e64b93a77663db42dc3e99">
<ol id="b-d8082fd2421047a09f758207d8471382">
<li id="b-90e73fd02c8c4edab229048bd272849a">与商家体验有关的骚扰内容</li>
</ol>
<ul id="b-68e27e6212724d378d24ad7e7c142ac1">
<li id="b-24aedd60ec0e4ce88ea4704d60073156"><span style="color: "><span style="font-size:14px">低能健身房真的是无休无止的打电话骚扰，不停打电话让你办卡。</span></span></li>
<li id="b-4a0944212a00455d8aae879842afa6a1"><span style="font-size:14px">垃圾商户，黑心商家。</span></li>
</ul>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-c51fefb9dda84be7a41e3dd75d6f9681">
<td id="b-a5b556fe4e0f4f6bad4f2cfe01d66909">
<p id="b-a0ec8748d09c406fb39c7d920039b176"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e4957795aa9848b1b04f1df8c63b21d2">
<h2 id="b-2d38c82581f24842894212223f00542f">内容无意义</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-f42f277585484d2aa009c7fc59e78ea7">
<p id="b-725a3a43c9b6409491ede2cec2a9e63a">无意义</p>
</td>
<td id="b-bffa3f53edbe4f52a26b3b439dac4e36">
<p id="b-1f69cf6ce04942dbb3d0d04d519cfc27"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8ab6db29343648e9b07c2ffa02f610df">
<p id="b-f449694fe75a4881a35d3e110acd7dd9"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-b48e018d592a4aa88c2d950ae8e742f9">
<li id="b-567c610ade0f44b9ad8317f1b313a493">无意义的文字、数字、标点符号、英文字母和特殊符号或者组合内容</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-d22567262c394302acd0561e4e1110f6"></p>
<p id="b-293078f81be84804830d5fa7a5c8c6a3"><span style="color:rgb(0, 186, 115)"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-54dbc649d1824cf0a10e7ba167d6792a">
<li id="b-483da1302587456488c9b29e28f8862a">纯表情不打标</li>
<li id="b-3aa02a561de5407dae64e98914e5e6d8">有意义的1111，2333，5555，6666不打标（无需区分数字数量），不在此范围的数字正常打标</li>
<li id="b-0ff927b25645446f8ac2d4ca43a101e6">有意义的数字（仅可通过）：+数字；例如+1、+10086等</li>
<li id="b-f557b1acec4546ed8755f40b92a326bb">有意义的文字：语气词或者连续的语气词以及有意义的回复，如“好”、“好的”、“行”、“好好好好”、“哈哈哈哈”、“啊啊啊啊”“嗯嗯嗯”等 不打标</li>
<li id="b-700319ab48f44540925f0c1abc36e1b2">外语&amp;方言，翻译后为有意义内容</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-255232f2faec416fb85a30ae3f64270d">
<p id="b-d400b5946f86415291a95f974443e61d"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c1c6e858723841a396e7c4c18c92cdf6">
<p id="b-68c240af877449d18b0877ed58087d24"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-38f06c7ef9914f95aa796ee37231c22c">
<p id="b-87fe2081da2a4959a01dad4cc69ae34d"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-82825c9b1d344410b0d7dc8aea65d986">
<td id="b-81c0982c9eb44c02a2348cc54a07883c">
<p id="b-8137f873787f40f8979b7dad3eb83561"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-d330b57a79c74ffba669b614b476a475">
<h2 id="b-2993f4e7d76146178cbd72581c554ba9">白事相关内容</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-3d1bcf5e389243ef9889014855aedc94">
<p id="b-1b68230440f8404a8fdc07c82eebdd79"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-7ad992df147f48dc8d8a6273550692dd">
<p id="b-0a4319b9b89d43c6ae555d913205f7e4"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-9ccb768be3274357ac96006ff4461a11">
<p id="b-e2f33ae34bb749eaa6e0857718527376"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-6c54ba44875d4ec88eb9a578f243198d">
<li id="b-946ae666fd01487dbf1fbfe214e2f983">出现丧葬的词见删，比如花圈、花圈寿衣、花圈纸钱、白色花圈、寿衣寿盒、纸钱、祭奠用品、纸钱元宝、纸元、墓地、墓碑等</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-459cfe54faad403793df43d76a98cfb8">
<p id="b-5dacfde3e6fa450db2682e2bb900f18c"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-7d6e3bda79004c839568212cf9c6dce6">
<p id="b-347af49f73b04efeb47afc5a0df98976"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-c78f37c58d6d43babef10bc2ed67993c">
<p id="b-fac52e283035416188b43bdb4e2cce41"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-0680e68badd544228db697fd474ed40a">
<td id="b-f15e61e9303949d0b09c4f0ef1ee4f74">
<p id="b-977bc3e9c4c34a109eed2edf5e7be033"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-1c08a8f179ff4164bcdf82222452c2ed">
<h2 id="b-e66a76440c514e15ad6f6bb952650268">餐饮无关图</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-1f3959b821ba4847a97b2cb213c80808">
<p id="b-23f65143207f43b99f02d74b34ac1c13"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e323ae7aa3944c3bbe521d4f4a194eda">
<p id="b-740de8e73cd04f39bff429eb6141676c"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8df78e2693224652ab9147f6831d073a">
<p id="b-de1565d5feb1475cb81fc1894c5ea57c"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-10db724f4304451f85532e4bb4fe42e8">
<li id="b-66ab7e09821748259d5ae012eaf1bf2c"><span style="color:#000">非商家门头、</span><span style="color:#000"><strong>餐饮店环境、采购单、</strong></span><span style="color:#000">用餐场景相关图片。</span></li>
<li id="b-7f674f74321f4d9ca445641c611a5983"><span style="color:#000">食品、饮品元素未在</span><span style="color:#000"><strong>餐饮店环境</strong></span><span style="color:#000">，且未占主体。</span></li>
</ol>
<details id="b-5c30790ac2cd400a8a9c7a96cf6d2002">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>餐饮店环境（适用于定义1、2）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-fe65d8d03ea8463c852c2ec6047afc1b">
<li id="b-684149bbd54443a5a1a607b3543c1569"><span style="color:#000">厨房：食材加工（需出现人员操作：菜品加工、切配、烹饪、备餐）、食材存放、清洁洗碗消毒区。</span></li>
<li id="b-ddea85fe7e644ad7a7365d12639dfc5f"><span style="color:#000">大堂：用餐区（公共区、包间）、点餐区、收银区、等位区、卫生间。</span></li>
<li id="b-ff5cde40b8a8422484df0d2cfc687a02"><span style="color:#000">其他区域：仓库、员工休息室。</span></li>
<li id="b-745c8788aaf8425291307f857102a296"><span style="color:#000">环境图中文字描述为餐饮店</span></li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-a69299d23ec741e787ad9e562ae20e5a">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>采购单（即餐饮店采购交易凭证，可泛化，适用于定义1）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-43e4c0f3d8de464eadb44d13b809600c"><span style="color:#000">报价单、采购单、 订单确认函、送货单、配送单、验收单、退货单、发票、对账单、付款凭证、质检报告、出库单、报关单、电子签章 / 电子回单（包含电子送货单、电子对账单、电子发票等）</span></p>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-8e0ee74e6bda49989855ee54241ce8ac"></p>
<p id="b-cec1754f1d504c61b4ce318d0b10362a"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>

<li id="b-f6b3c3bda6c04ed6bc580c81ffa6e1a1">线下实拍图可通过。</li>
</ol>

<td id="b-499b63eeea404e16815d2367874ade02">
<h2 id="b-5afe3587847c496b8fcdeaf76151d8db">AI生成门头图</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-81b274dca8704abdb07f66aef1e3eba4">
<p id="b-6acbf78f76554c48a450a8dae4a2e110"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e997c643f0a14eac86cd9598fac4e09c">
<p id="b-2125519a576f4dabb0d6e6205504315c"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-6f365ee66d08430d8491248fe9ad15c8">
<p id="b-812b3c07b97c4b5f81768a20455e9d63"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-e329e1f8bc404ee7b56f30b4a821abec">
<li id="b-d6cae372698b4d71afa83841e3309444">
<p id="b-32a7353594614bd4a48bd08b01b818eb">展示AI生成非真实拍摄的商家门店信息画面（从松管控）</p>
<ul id="b-02bb9c9dfa4d475c9e3ea9af135f5fde">
<li id="b-db992f9633444427b025c5109a23df15">
<p id="b-dc352e7e61984f659d1affa1685f7125">判断点：（包括但不限于）</p>
<ul id="b-7692861ad99842648b28df82897cb8ef">
<li id="b-30894dbdacba4888be0a8d21b324a61c">出现错字</li>
<li id="b-dd00b820bb544d36838235e46c9d3dda">人物等画面元素出现非常理的异常形态或行为不符合逻辑</li>
<li id="b-4c9ae20f7ccd4be2810438305859a6cf">画面质感在纹理细节上存在模糊感或同质化，非真实拍摄场景（如墙面的斑驳痕迹、布料的纤维纹理、食物的细腻纹路等）</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-9273e828ac5840d8b0f6298746d1578b"><span style="color:#FF4A47">备注：100981也需参考此标准</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-76e25c3916864a1d9b6d7f6c077a418e">
<p id="b-13056de4a321402c88d957f4fe12004b"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-83ced9b9d95f44fa9201d2c33f0f284f">
<ol id="b-eea35984ba734d1f9f7955a8423d7354">
<li id="b-6d5e8dd183f04b698fca08dbe6518a34">
<p id="b-62f69cf0ae6a44eb9009b8400536a700">错字</p>
<ul id="b-1fcdd39c8dba4c05a1cefd94043e9201">
<li id="b-90de406448c24edb9defb209beb05d7c"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726101388?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236728343944?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-0b34245fbd524fe5b1ba1190fca7ecc5">
<p id="b-26d1629e4c1742b194079bc17120c1f7">画面元素不符逻辑</p>
<ul id="b-43a6da61cd794c358e7ff08f3e23a2a6">
<li id="b-b8be7a2a7fc94c6799d78a34c3aae740"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726260237?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726416976?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236727707244?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-c6c812b1924a4f1fac26696ad72cff8d">
<p id="b-0dcaabf548a64b228d3626be18bc6eb7">非真实拍摄场景</p>
<ul id="b-a17b9a4fc84448259877e30931ff78a2">
<li id="b-12ee9bdcf56f40a497492251b5ccd923"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236729825414?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-cb78167358814fa6bb7205bad97cfb18">
<p id="b-104f26a6c2ec46ebb13fe1f65a3358bc"></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-f4195d20adb64e08a18da94feffc9fa1">
<td id="b-6ea849d7d2234c728473671f8485b745">
<p id="b-3787966b1249407e854d111ffe0efd62"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-545d795d302d4ec18b20834212f4a9fc">
<h2 id="b-ef6043ceb5264fa0bd1dfa478e8066d8">菜品非实拍</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-b4b58d46cfc54cf3b03bf388bd91dc3f">
<p id="b-ea36af99e9f8460b80ec3e889e8cc3ce"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-208d01ef1c0f4260b30da14c3534ec49">
<p id="b-4d83bbed7c384f0784c044a688305911"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-d5bf844891cf4d1584ec1ae6549fe8e5">
<p id="b-2ceeb20429ea466b99c103e2c79e4c80"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-41d486aa7ce744eb9b846654c3025ed7"><span style="color:#000">非线下实拍的食品、饮品图，需打标。</span></p>
<ol id="b-82b90055f96949c1a5c2087f5e4a4d0c">
<li id="b-c4cbf4155e8e491c9f573206211a36a3"><span style="color:#000">经过二次创作的菜品、饮品的宣传图、海报。</span></li>
<li id="b-f6ad205511b44e1f852682f585ebc038">写真风格、漫画/简笔画风格、带有AI生成标识的食品、饮品图。</li>
<li id="b-fa8b6f99493048fdaf8e8d0d79e2a014">
<p id="b-ad3177517a7b468aa854bac89263f401">食品、饮品图片中出现带边框的图片、截图截屏，具体判断方式如下：</p>
<ul id="b-a3bfabc8d71a49f7ba8834b31d2cf7ed">
<li id="b-5924cc577c4540d794acd3d1bff7a2b7"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><del>带有大众点评水印/logo的，无需点开图片或通过图片缩放的形式，出现带边框的图片、截图截屏的</del></span></li>
<li id="b-f4b8b8cd1b5e4d3c8212c4fe39de6b23">
<p id="b-8fb7baffa7c04907974c4d08a3d07bc4">没有大众点评水印/logo的，无需点开图片或通过图片缩放的形式，查看边框的颜色、图案、宽窄与图片明显不符的</p>
<p id="b-e629004b3d9741d7a11699aea6e1cb56">备注：无需区分边框颜色、图案、宽窄</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p id="b-8bc2b4a5f3054a95a1a4668559b5b8b4"></p>
</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-704ce3112d7241af933930fe1bd255ef"></p>
<p id="b-d45f2ebbe81848cbb5ec09deeab19b7e"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-739d87069035417289ff1c06c2c270e5">
<li id="b-819c438f838e4927992395555406ecba">食品、饮品的线下实拍图</li>
<li id="b-1bd42342bb6a467788857a914087546a">
<p id="b-2644ab14ed3d4d6a852ecf9baf29c38d">食品、饮品的线下实拍图中，仅有大众点评的水印/logo，且出现<strong>二次创作元素、</strong>写真风格或<span style="color:#FF4A47">带边框的图片、截图截屏</span>。</p>
<details id="b-f572e987490d4eed9a734ecaafd850f2">
<summary><strong>二次创作元素（可泛化，适用于定义2）</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-96ccf91c64a941c59904d6f3fe3f1ee3">贴纸、图标、文字艺术字等</p>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-75e1d5875bf8496ba44976d4a4a0a1cf">线下实拍的餐饮店环境中出现食品、饮品元素。</li>
<li id="b-a062d5aa1d0042bd9565163404b50d16">
<p id="b-74b07776535c497ab29d049841ba63ad"><span style="color:#000">无“类目”字段或类目字段为【-】【空】时，无需判断是否为非实拍。（type:100469不执行此条豁免项）</span></p>
<p id="b-e5bf06dbe39d4e8ba90c73374e824fea"></p>
</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-747a2eb5b7e140338a2c8fb5b98a6bf6">
<p id="b-1cd6d490dd904af2ad23e96d1466ca68"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-76384ae1d71c485680163696e88faae9">
<ol id="b-6f11cf5fa2894c9c99c54526d1980e5d">
<li id="b-3dc9208fd8e7458ab621abe54930873b">
<p id="b-5843f44cc32c459baf2c3506c7d52127">宣传图、海报</p>
<ul id="b-bd9f06961eb942ac8cd91282245eefbf">
<li id="b-bf97202180dd422f9dae0c9e7e39173a"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236729354446?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236729194905?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726106725?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-8479fa422c4744ff911cfe4ab4c58e64">
<p id="b-031a3352a3244b20994d3cb4a3783c43">写真风格</p>
<ul id="b-92941720f3a94018922a26e8079721b8">
<li id="b-4722dbd58265498c83c62752c89bfc87"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236729515090?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726582638?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236725947798?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-248a1733fcd84712b83e5f2ee88d9b2e">
<p id="b-e7bb4cc147964167b74cbea02c47143a">漫画、简笔画</p>
<ul id="b-dbbbd048daa74696aac0d07decc1eefd">
<li id="b-170d09aee2a445d79c571e61096e2c91"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236728671583?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236729354367?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-2782e66cd2864115a168c730ed5507f3">
<p id="b-d1a1630a69ec4d249eac48cd780b53ea">边框</p>
<ul id="b-d8d0077abaa048b0bc7117f6b35922df">
<li id="b-a745be18471e4ae4b3b0fba7de5bb8a3"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236728877218?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-bd95bb988507410e9af0eb4052bcb49f">
<p id="b-d46885258e20406e879a2eba9b147aae">截图</p>
<ul id="b-f1d91377aa6947e7890302bd31951958">
<li id="b-a058395e458f4fb0bbef4d694202136b"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236725418252?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726265512?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-8ac4f7016f1f47bd946568d7d46b2eeb">
<p id="b-8b33bff8024f4fe2ae181d9ea1f71a73">无大众点评水印、logo，图片上下左右篇幅出现明显黑框的</p>
<ul id="b-81f9a016137d47399d555032e3513fc2">

</td>
<td id="b-5c31442125dc4b179df00a2d6bcddced">
<h2 id="b-681db0ed9e23453593e538e143fd4313">环境非实拍</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-2a9095f304664dcf9dd0ab79176dffd7">
<p id="b-fda02f1f76b44d4da2110ed495d4c881"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-576f75f913764b648c8cebd3cb999374">
<p id="b-cb315851d2694faa968770e6df509c96"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-64b940989fcf405a82f596206c0c8dbf">
<p id="b-6b0865c1d6f54a2d82d111b6b4f2ef54"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-e5250e3cdb3245ffb03d2a74b7692a6f">
<li id="b-0c4402581a1a4f2d9b1acd0bd8f5cce4">
<p id="b-c9c2d477878e44af8bf18b135871660d">非线下实拍的<strong>餐饮店环境</strong>图</p>
<details id="b-e108697bd50f4541a4b21febfb6a9e79">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>餐饮店环境（适用于定义1）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-f26e9fffe12a442a83ba7435d5b43773">
<li id="b-82ef1892adf14ab3a5a4fc07260e4c43">厨房：食材加工（需出现人员操作：菜品加工、切配、烹饪、备餐）、食材存放、清洁洗碗消毒区。</li>
<li id="b-b348d1faa1be42009689a3fd50969c4e">大堂：用餐区（公共区、包间）、点餐区、收银区、等位区、卫生间。</li>
<li id="b-cee4f41e975c49e3aa09f8be12904fdc">其他区域：仓库、员工休息室。</li>
<li id="b-70c1975406314721bc648c350dec0016"><span style="color:#000">环境</span>图中文字描述为餐饮店</li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-919578ef2adb46eb85779c91e970ca80">经过二次创作的餐饮店环境宣传图、海报等。</li>
<li id="b-d50cf0fb77114f26aaab78e566688aa2">写真风格、漫画/简笔画风格<span style="color:#000">、带有AI生成标识的餐</span>饮店环境图。</li>
<li id="b-e358b73f5867469a982e34ae2cc363d3">AI生成的非真实拍摄餐饮店环境图（出现错字、人物等画面元素出现非常理的异常形态或行为不符合逻辑、画面质感在纹理细节上存在模糊感或同质化，非真实拍摄等场景）。</li>
<li id="b-d350d83adcf34bd9927f9383548e90af"><span style="color:#000">餐饮店环境图片出现带边框的图片（无需区分边框颜色、图案、宽窄）、截图截屏。</span></li>
<li id="b-eef7b14f272b4cadb50b3207ff6a911d"><span style="color:#000">打码、遮挡食材存放、食材加工场景。</span></li>
</ol>
<p id="b-bfa0799219914c00879c06fa666e9f5f">备注：100981也需参考此标准</p>
<p id="b-c0f8170f7a4d4acdb286740505258c08"></p>
<p id="b-3261a3bcd9f549efa0f8ec77d34e7140"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-b10201345e30470595684734fa6ecbc9">
<li id="b-227cafaec09a43b7a4b715b2cfc87de4"><span style="color:#000">餐饮店环境的线下实拍图。</span></li>
<li id="b-97d14e8767874155aa452297d0013091">
<p id="b-b88c3ad2dae7400cbc1a4e0ff48dd230"><span style="color:#000">餐饮店环境的线下实拍图中仅有大众点评的水印/logo，且出现</span><span style="color:#000"><strong>二次创作元素</strong></span><span style="color:#000">。</span></p>
<details id="b-3193ad9c36384c6da73284c6ba0e61cc">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>二次创作元素（可泛化，适用于定义2）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-6cc798727bda45e4bd60ce8a7c964a62">贴纸、图标、文字艺术字等</p>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-8e6e12c484d849d9bf77a7fad659a65a"><span style="color:#000">类目字段为空白内容，图片为实拍餐饮店环境，且出现各类二次创作素材（宣传图、海报、二次创作元素）</span>。（100469不参考此条豁免项）</li>
<li id="b-aebeb24858c04a269115f4aaa35137b4">类目字段为空白内容，图片为实拍餐饮店环境，且出现带边框的图片（无需区分边框颜色、图案、宽窄）或截图截屏。（100469不参考此条豁免项）</li>
<li id="b-aa56c8f7dcd54c5fa4e89bf08a0b997c">
<p id="b-a90386f855854c2292ad800006b82813"><span style="color:#FF4A47">美团官方发布的</span><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>放心消费平台海报</strong></span></p>
<details id="b-535725c5a61e43719ed4cee7789afe46">
<summary><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>放心消费平台海报（</strong></span><span style="color:#FF4A47">适用于定义5</span><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-05c61b8839cb48bbbbbc70649081f1fa"><span style="color:#FF4A47">不限制图片尺寸格式，内容需一致</span></p>
<p id="b-dd4e63e66583452c96371ccab1bd396c"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/261216293380?contentType=1&amp;compress=@471w_1l">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/261216296029?contentType=1&amp;compress=@789w_1l">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-a63feb7c38994123aa97532c017a8ab6"></p>
</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-8ba15fb2a4a14f6184a5cd8a2b9f60ec"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-878cca101f4a457483763e8ab1ad2991">
<p id="b-49899e738a4642e9a23a630620706950"></p>
</td>

<td id="b-53202c68d31f4a668e90aaabf3466d2b">
<ol id="b-0ec0475a62e846539cecbd3bbb3dd716">
<li id="b-101c5e7247c54ba6b189a61566494f25">
<p id="b-30347b865a18474b9f60d2ef49cf4da0">餐饮店环境的线下实拍图</p>
<p id="b-f4e6a9ab1a854e44a9d599c7dc218f7d"></p>
<p id="b-a0b2e293569c47eab72d268a45ca1080"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236729199917?&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726587596?&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726270450?&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236725636177?&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726746904?&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236730631411?&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236728361202?&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</li>
<li id="b-c273338d73d94ebe9ccfcbb0f8a4765e">
<p id="b-fe2f612cb3df4382865a2515764172bf"><span style="color:#FF4A47">美团官方发布的放心消费平台海报</span></p>
<p id="b-c446a62a85414a2ca9d723258033cff8">​<span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/261216293380?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/261216296029?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</li>
</ol>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-6b323a899d1543349e0f8ba98a8fabe6">
<td id="b-66a95f0aee4445a0956fbbe4e8b27886">
<p id="b-13b84c940d534536a9466f8ebe607754"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-05b3a237a4b642b8ac4ddde9d206f598">
<h2 id="b-7edd68b57b1d4c0f8be1ca9859cc44fa">食材采购违规</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-bef5077a8d4b49efa724df58ca2a92b6">
<p id="b-289128dd4aa64583bad41d387f211775"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-429b03d751fd430896c6211edf6d93a6">
<p id="b-99567d166683457c9f103b9643526307"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8bbbdd9e3f9d4f74b9a219f1dd4a0ee4">
<p id="b-2262ffc5d1604f929b5987db71cef746"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-469b060f0b47449bad8c771c5d945579">
<li id="b-11cc97b0274442e1a63b851a2dadc4a3"><span style="color:#000">打码、遮挡、裁切、涂改采购单。</span></li>
<li id="b-cc788e61f1974da6bfada2c79a0b71f1">
<p id="b-fb72a94344da466d9beb49df7a5e23a4"><span style="color:#000">采购单无关键信息：商品名称&amp;日期（月/日）。</span></p>
<ul id="b-eee8649778ec40168d3083ca4681e044">
<li id="b-c15c77cb8dd04bf9b3fb748815aee5a6"><span style="color:#000">日期（月/日）模糊、无法识别，需打标。</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-7f5abeebade94bbb9ae3d0b9e1b4a0c8">
<p id="b-dea5da15fd8c463ab1cdc37f2181f4ba"><span style="color:#000">采购单日期（月/日）截止审核日期，大于一个月。</span></p>
<ul id="b-b5975d517294423c9ad55fcf9e52180c">
<li id="b-4aceee9100f945678a00085975bfcd8a"><span style="color:#000">采购单中出现多个日期，以最早的日期核算，截止审核日期，大于一个月。</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-46b9de3313244c97b970ee31999646e3">
<p id="b-368a960dc3594e4c8acf6101d7489846"><span style="color:#000"><strong>采购单</strong></span><span style="color:#000">中出现水印/logo。</span></p>
<details id="b-9c3b0c2322da48df880f77c7efbb1f86">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>采购单（即餐饮店采购交易凭证，可泛化，适用于定义4）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-b48a1efc582745a998c7fcbf2505a0bf" style="text-align:start"><span style="color:#000">报价单、采购单、 订单确认函、送货单、配送单、验收单、退货单、发票、对账单、付款凭证、质检报告、出库单、报关单、电子签章 / 电子回单（包含电子送货单、电子对账单、电子发票等）</span></p>
</div>
</details>
</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-d78b708b6e68467aa31e1f92e8d31310"><span style="color:#FF4A47">备注：100981也需参考此标准</span></p>
<p id="b-d700815cbb1748768d3cf3e749fe1d8b"></p>
<p id="b-93b4094c311f48038dde883cf3dd8aa0"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-5bdba64f6a0846039c0089799d1f9df0">
<li id="b-346cdfe0987442d9acf399a570367b11"><span style="color:#000">采购单中出现水印/logo仅豁免大众点评、手机拍摄水印（照片上添加的手机型号、文字、日期、地点等信息）</span></li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-53966811cca04e40a25d200b4130a4a8">
<p id="b-9bf5bc9054fa4e639349ac7283db1ec6"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-43809b163dee47908ae8ad85e996fce3">
<ol id="b-7d344b21954a4e9a965f93c8d29146de">
<li id="b-a2b5676153ad453893e1bdf37d073df9">
<p id="b-0258b7c0fa0c4302b60dd51c602ffff4">过有效期</p>
<ul id="b-b273f77d3752462cb3d16a3c42f88007">
<li id="b-746d2bcb998847b7937089a12d7cdc3b"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726120456?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-3bd5cbb4a1f94765b7cca3984449c0fc">
<p id="b-51d38b8aee8c4a85ac6653ed2e63ceff">违规涂改</p>
<ul id="b-842455fce183419587557ed5fb142e55">
<li id="b-a192a27b10b44460989b1c5384684bff"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236727100249?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-2f9176912e1c4c04ae092997bcc80662">
<ol id="b-c26a246e469b4d4096120b6df277abff">
<li id="b-34d6d4bdd9a24810bf315d31098118fb">
<p id="b-fdae8ab9518543e1b26f1b482c046490"><span style="color:#000">采购单中出现水印/logo仅豁免大众点评、手机拍摄水印</span></p>
<ul id="b-9b00a1a6ec9245b9b943ef34f82c3502">
<li id="b-12b288afb07b42798b74157703cfdcdb"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236730164433?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-a89417bdc2a843949981d2d1da2b1df0">
<td id="b-7929fef2663b4963a3b8f5ec9c55dbaf">
<p id="b-bfc73b7df8724892bc978dffcea332d8"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-611843db3c1d4a86a6c8b127c3fe14fb">
<h2 id="b-f9b06d355955448b88fbd7e33cf88751">相册类目错放</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-e3b3ab021d1c44f1be1e6720a1c189e9">
<p id="b-da2d25e14f444dc09e1a0e66a1b83be9"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-54d61572e23a4fd4b51029d86316b8a7">
<p id="b-598555de4b3b49959868fdae0243114a"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-1ccd6b57b3de47a088959065d02d25cd">
<p id="b-e39d4b08c531456d92c69c176991c913"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-f2d7ffc677034c55bfd4e4eaed3796d1">
<li id="b-3eb96df27019447caa8af94fe8a938eb">
<p id="b-86469b0e993544dba953cde54821f606"><span style="color:#000">图中餐饮店元素、场景与</span><span style="color:#000"><strong>类目</strong></span><span style="color:#000">不一致。</span></p>
<details id="b-c2f6e8ec981b4821a883c27a8ea3b0aa">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>类目（适用于定义1）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-2ed538d840254d8187456ab0096e5c28"><span style="color:#000">环境其他、菜品类、食材加工、门面、大堂、厨房、食材采购、食材存放</span></p>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-ec18a4dcc525481e8dc43faf10bff937"><span style="color:#000">食品、饮品非主体展示。</span></li>
<li id="b-dbf6298b3ff448ebb48c5fbefdfddb55"><span style="color:#000">无法识别为类目相关的图片。</span></li>
</ol>
<p id="b-dc6d6aa4288b4c699fc842edc42b51e6"><span style="color:#FF4A47">备注：100981也需参考此标准</span></p>
<p id="b-09ea0650bbcd4c049a166ec2170ec8a3"></p>
<p id="b-455ced72f95d4340adb0e46b512e0df6"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-4bb6aaa1f955465b8f7209901aba61bb">
<li id="b-75d51cdb06aa42ad926c908a830527fd"><span style="color:#000">任一场景、元素与类目</span><span style="color:#FF4A47">相关</span><span style="color:#000">，可通过。</span></li>
<li id="b-484fe2ae7eb947b1b82a91d2d719d838"><span style="color:#000">无类目字段，无需与类目核对一致。</span></li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-6575f854e8824edcbc0f602e05fa7278">
<p id="b-9f2942a9419a4e1390851689fb64f50d"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-08c0b86381a749c4842daf844d96a052">
<ol id="b-f58cc62cd72d47399f560b535f2abca3">
<li id="b-08b66cf571204a299d2df273bc6847c2">
<p id="b-8b2bcc2c431f423bb7f83cdf02f4cd2c">无法识别为类目相关的图片</p>
<ul id="b-ab684f7e87db4aed95a39a3239371c2c">
<li id="b-ab54f4e874a3463688376648b942f764"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726602166?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726602168?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-d5d74ac7cf2a4595b23ace6f2f9b24f4">
<ol id="b-b163a59933cc4a128e71132d6d14d50b">
<li id="b-a69ee686130e47ebb1a5434a48af3f0e">
<p id="b-95f289e3764948079187f4af44f8348a">类目：大堂</p>
<ul id="b-6c65766bbee049649f47562ea9eaaad7">
<li id="b-3517d575b3284321b5aa414311093324"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236730170290?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-9af90d110645428f937b55abc1098af2">
<p id="b-a7f8928830c54538af3835ac110d5c43">类目：后厨图</p>
<ul id="b-de71f724f8a5473cbf06b5b75d5e9c9b">
<li id="b-c761f20712e04e709be2c82d076dcd6b"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236730645827?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236726441612?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-f241be5263cc4b11bb2c0b6020ec5645">
<p id="b-cdb18676c06f4cd79c402579b851f42a">类目：食材加工</p>
<ul id="b-fe802bebcf214b7a94350c46dd1bfc07">
<li id="b-ebfaca4169d6425f8c57ec1752d96d76"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236730915021?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236728383459?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-c5068d388d444dd3b1473fa4f5bf0935">任一场景、元素与类目相关</li>
</ol>
<details id="b-fda29575d2e64260ae2abc108416a231">
<summary>考虑到纯外卖商家的实际情况，只要场景元素与类目相关即可豁免</summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-f83dc13d96aa437dbb58e1c915f2c041">
<li id="b-b9c0c882b33141908d34a5ad7716b29a">
<p id="b-35b3401cd0d340fcb09a5d7f83e2daa1">类目：食材加工</p>
<p id="b-93b7145578954c98807d2c8acd69646f"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/254153142810?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/254159264459?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/254153142808?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</li>
<li id="b-c24fe19f3ce044b8b286359a04d663e0">
<p id="b-a9e9a9fd30a54b449014692a64520bbc">类目：大堂</p>
<p id="b-dda99f91bd0a4919b66234a710973e3c"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/254159224417?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="b-117fa1405ade4774a6fc214c37d64752">
<td id="b-c07ea7827f4d4eadbcd033c403499108">
<p id="b-9bc6a8f86c94492aa562ebb73b3c415b"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-e0e58f3d022348aea4b3d1a46c29fa6d">
<h2 id="b-75517b5026f74ee18817ce929182a08b">友商平台</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-5d51de58565e4b7a8c1573d1db413d65">
<p id="b-6175267ef3994fcfaa1522e01ac74fb8"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-88c40acb236f46b78e455f3b48b3c9d1">
<p id="b-32e0137bf8c240849df48e358550f05f"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-14c7be08e0ee42b78589156b66ad78c0">
<p id="b-bc0a6907b7fc41f19fd00ce2343f9a65"><span style="color:rgb(255, 74, 71)"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-02aa6bc2cbf64c2e8592a9228d2accae">
<li id="b-d6917247cfa240208b0e01079c7c316f">线上图片涉及<strong>三方平台水印/logo、</strong>联系方式、网址。</li>
<li id="b-d12942e1ff6e4082bdb5702a7d88cfee">
<p id="b-be04d08564ae41f9be6ff4305d91bb3b"><span style="color:#FF4A47">线下图片涉及第</span><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>三方平台水印/logo</strong></span><span style="color:#FF4A47">。</span></p>
<details id="b-3aa96c030083479aae4b4e3491ec6b93">
<summary><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>三方平台（适用于定义1、2）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-7ecc867e130e4bb59c81346065801fc6">
<li id="b-b8554d28395f43269f7de0d824c5a42a"><span style="color:#FF4A47">涉及第三方平台，无需区分是否为核心区域，出现即打击：高德、高徳扫街榜、高德状元榜</span></li>
<li id="b-8f7aa46da6d9402ea3c93e34302ef6b9"><span style="color:#FF4A47">涉及第三方外卖平台宣传语，无需区分是否为核心区域，出现即打击：京东外卖、饿了么（淘宝闪购/淘宝外卖/闪购外卖）</span></li>
<li id="b-b724c74d6d5c48eab741aba28b39f02f"><span style="color:#FF4A47">涉及其他第三方平台，需判断是否为核心区域：京东、抖音、快手、小红书、外卖取餐处（京东外卖/淘宝闪购/淘宝外卖/闪购外卖）</span></li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-4d999dbec12341949a6222b005134e8c">线上涉及<strong>人名及称谓、肖像及形象</strong>。</li>
<li id="b-a69ff3acabf84d94be182473718ebf9b">
<p id="b-162048baa7b348088b292f9bf0678058">线下图片<strong>核心区域</strong>涉及<strong>人名及称谓、肖像及形象</strong>。</p>
<details id="b-f47417826c9a48be9e513d192b2e4a02">
<summary><strong>人名及称谓、肖像及形象（适用于定义3、4）</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-4cb2b32d5bc34f7e93bc0a5124c38871">人名及称谓：刘强东、张一鸣、马云、蒋凡、王兴、罗永浩</p>
<p id="b-0d1f181fcfdc449bb788f9b84fab6ab2">肖像及形象：人物真人照片、卡通形象、剪影、签名样式</p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-a045119cd9d044d2b891fc6e19c4b6b2">
<summary><strong>核心区域（适用于定义3、4）</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-05099e9365e44c0c803638098da6fc6a">图片核心区域具体定义：<br>1. 水平范围：<br>从图像左侧起25%到右侧75%的宽度。<br>2. 垂直范围：<br>从图像顶部起25%到底部75%的高度。</p>
<p id="b-e1142465e8564bd391886a3a85d7cba1">3.风险内容同时横跨核心区域与非核心区域时，可视为命中核心区域。</p>
<p id="b-9d8447dbf0874372b9bf66d1627ef79b"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236725138617?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-a7a562a67406485b927f1e4c760a29f2">线上/线下图片出现千问logo的</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-5281efe63a9143a6bf031db7dae3d4f5"><span style="color:#000">备注：100981也需参考此标准</span></p>
<p id="b-4b51b38047714ea9a2b86eb85155a5ed"></p>
<p id="b-3a1a4579675b4e36b9167087ca155e1c"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-5ef278a6988640499bb66bb3e54fb265">
<li id="b-667f1092e4814f37bf26a389c31f97bf">采购单中出现联系方式和网址。</li>
<li id="b-6a1ecaa457004977858d048b10d66990">提及商家店铺名称涉及人名及称谓，与关联信息的商户名完全一致的。</li>
<li id="b-bb7255f6a4fb47ae9b4e15bfdd7f8c03">线上/线下图片出现千问水印标识、千问AI生成的</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-f6e1cfa2fadd4cb4a25e099e508c1da4">
<p id="b-90a7a45ac8714e899683a5bb88471220"></p>
</td>

<li id="b-d7f1c2e2be8f40c99c3cda16cc0ba8e2">

<tr id="b-e781aa0e5ad94fc5b605b6e647c7f56c">
<td id="b-055404b8d7d54a7aaa87c3f70be6043f">
<p id="b-9934ef718e314920bf503e39074a74ea"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-97e9c9053ed24f67924a0cf0680b8192">
<h2 id="b-2fbee0fa787f4d5dab9931307531ae9e">非真实门头图</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-96c40a89502f4564bc96907720e25b15">
<p id="b-59cea36610f94d4ca62eeae9b81798e9"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-25fe813dac0748a0adb3d89ba3b0a78b">
<p id="b-c6f6920cd8db4c50bd4d28c447397bd2"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-1c8db77e56a6415a9007eefddb0f803b">
<p id="b-abd38929088244f48928e6e8936fdbf2"><span style="color:#000"><strong>【图/视】</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-d9824e9fbceb4efbb89062f0149b32b4"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-1dda1cc54d384cde94fec5e96536149f">管控前提：【100981-外卖商家端装修相册审核】type点、类目为【<span style="color:#FF4A47">门面</span>】才需按照以下标准进行审核</p>
<p id="b-0b8aa3b492654d2a949ecacdc8a76aaa">审核逻辑（适用于定义1）：</p>
<ol id="b-e25f466322f14c9dbbed0a7bb40c7d51">
<li id="b-17ad89dc679147d1b6572b6c63b6f8d0">有对比图片，仅需图图对比，无需进行视频对比</li>
<li id="b-9fc6debecdc947f685e436fdc37b4a93">仅出现1张图片的，需结合下方视频进行对比，判断是否为同一门店内容<span style="color:#000">（比</span>对视频最多看前10s）</li>
<li id="b-52097c7826d747deafd3aff8878b5bbe">图片视频：仅有图片或视频，无需审核</li>
<li id="b-ffbd4148b89b4c929307012b183a6033">被审视频：出现该提示内容，视频需要全程看完，判断是否为同一门店内容</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-a863a7bdc5324a9c860c35607d7ce4c2">具体标准：</p>
<ol id="b-3942bf05f3ed4393ba81b851f85f2849">
<li id="b-d578a922628841e682ed839161bedfbe">
<p id="b-1a364f0812174763b2266d05cbaa3059">涉及商家相册对比信息时，审核方式如下：</p>
<ul id="b-cd346e55661b4f2ab1a9d55a2083446b">
<li id="b-db32171c590e467aac4bab2af6a05287">
<p id="b-c290d3de71d84fffae093740954a9639">商家店铺名称、门头信息（门面其他信息、logo等）、招牌底色、周边环境四个维度的相似程度低于1/2（即不足一半相似）的，需打标</p>
<ul id="b-2d6883fcd9ec46068366ab8b44e2a138">
<li id="b-2ba888d27c514e1c9b61e3b960dcf910">1/2定义：四维度有2个维度及其以上的，即可判定大于1/2</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-6d42f1614f2a4fdfb601472587c4ee73">
<p id="b-f546cf4a7b9b457d9978453805ba5769" style="text-align:start">若存在明显P图、AI生成或非同一门店信息的情形，需打标</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-b2c911e5e5444f03aa743050dbb07269">备注：审核标准：变更为V3通用标准叠加商户相册标准</p>
<p id="b-d1768c28a389458980398ccaae749110"></p>
<p id="b-8bb76fb42c0142ec9c53b41b41708de6"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-a4acf7349b3746229fe68b4540dd76b4">
<li id="b-2d80b5e49bfe43558da1d28e749a036a">商家店铺环境周边布局一样的，仅店铺信息存在不一致的，可豁免</li>
</ol>
</td>
<td id="b-add0a13abce74b13bdeeacfd9f747d84">
<p id="b-4d821e1ff449411db95cb2166920384c"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-34c1d28140dd4e889c4fb1581aa79355">
<ol id="b-c82851fb7a714b4b83fd1fa6c3ecebb4">
<li id="b-717de0ec2c764e3aae3a686ff0b0a830">
<p id="b-15c80711d74a47d39d2263229dd8e2ef">对比图片的商家门头信息、招牌底色等完全不一致</p>
<ul id="b-166ea69dcc0c48809287aba76d72071e">
<li id="b-32a374c7a96847a3b73d571ab0a55714"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/237663372223?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/237667348862?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-cd95394fbed6452c829c0d8489cac8e2">
<p id="b-f09904c52ae34ad9a11ee364c2b3fffc">商家招牌底色（金色与红色）、店铺及周边环境（店铺外、堂食空间、排队位置）不一致，相似程度&lt;1/2</p>
<ul id="b-f4b5e81b69474bd0af895d2a8429aaf1">
<li id="b-af970db5f1f84134a4c57f0f5abab424"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/237667029545?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/237671502661?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-37c08faad4544a3f8c496cdac8616e80">
<p id="b-a7252edace6d47018642dea198caa14c">仅出现一张商家店铺图片，结合下方视频内容判断并非同一门店</p>

<td id="b-e4dbb7c1c25e4f92a5269bd19d49355f">
<h2 id="b-0485d78e133647c8a1cc5ff95cd426d3">虚假堂食环境</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-3f8d447dc0ba4f53a216b9da34891c34">
<p id="b-897b7d556abc4874b152bfdba77a7cb8"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-3ce388be90dd447b837f5146671ed7c4">
<p id="b-076d831a4c204d19a57ac15cee50bdb9"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-4176d2fd1eea4ee39fd1cdcd3b19d70d">
<p id="b-547b5fa600aa4720a3e9d82aff5fc852"><span style="color:#000"><strong>【图/视】</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-70d20f273ead4835be997bb147980a4d"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-9cb4463ffbe945edba3e64946214448a">管控前提：仅【100981-外卖商家端装修相册审核】type点生效以下标准</p>
<ol id="b-34c421cc7c414dedbc86f6b1e5a167e8">
<li id="b-0042ae5a3fac42229e1954011acae755">
<p id="b-7c647478d5924fb5b9b8046e2ef23e20">图片信息字段提示为“是否为纯外卖”时，纯外卖商家审核方式如下：</p>
<ul id="b-6a4e29af59a24321bedef8fc6fdea915">
<li id="b-7fe6495cfa6c483c8cfa75f759ec417a">图片信息中直接出现“可堂食”“支持堂食”“堂食店”等明确表示可堂食字样或变体描述的</li>
<li id="b-8dbbbb9f58504e2f9d1f94f04a273da6">
<p id="b-5b0e1be7d8b64cc4b4f2d6758a6dc0ed">展示店铺环境且出现4张及以上（含4张）的4人桌的</p>
<ul id="b-96f371cec6914e39acfcca748ab32c7f">
<li id="b-570cbb6dae2d444ea7cd5851bf41da29">若展示桌椅局部照片，可放宽要求，无需计入桌椅数量审核</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li id="b-f00b8c92249a4228a250b3b51d07d120"><span style="color:#FF4A47">同时命中其他自定义标签时，按照标签优先级审核</span></li>
</ol>
<p id="b-104ba7e1da4241dab57eaddb81840013"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><del>备注：审核标准：变更为V3通用标准叠加商户相册标准</del></span></p>
<p id="b-6bacaf5c3d0f4d599dd8b60f1ebcfb36"></p>
<p id="b-aaea19cd4a9a47dfafde37bb2348fd29"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-ba1c3872d55d4f00894edc71f9a99700">
<li id="b-145d886d2f704b6e94f63b5fea221bc5">纯外卖商家店铺环境出现吧台桌、单人椅等非4人桌的，可豁免</li>
</ol>
</td>

<h2 id="b-e542e0ae02a14a7a80c547ea4325816f">评价无关美女图片</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-38e2e984aca4442b9ee4f81a2273e585">
<p id="b-f061c6e09b3e4e7d83e6c200a5338c50"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-859afa3256bc4bc994065fbf64773827">
<p id="b-cf46507ee7a144cc97d7c6374f412e3e"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-5bb7cf77153e42d5bb79d18b0727f0c1">
<p id="b-83ee5b1fda0745b88a076c71513b6e49"><span style="color:#000"><strong>【图/视】</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-4bc56dc1454c41a69e3c4bc5e5f5fba9">管控品类：按摩/足疗、私人影院、新奇体验、<strong>娱乐会所</strong>品类</p>
<p id="b-d74728a5a4094a4e90f848accb28ed95"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-37d16959549c4eeb97815a8b824c50ad">
<li id="b-b947b25baf0e440990d2bc1b4aebeb28">根据商户名及品类判断与实际商户业务无关，女性占主体或仅出现一名女性，且无法<strong>明确身份。</strong></li>
</ol>
<details id="b-bfbf79009ee148759e99a10f772379b0">
<summary><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>明确身份</strong></span><strong>（适用于定义1）</strong></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-23b1d5be4c24403bb829da4466690ab7"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>不满足以下条件时可从严</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-1413346e2c864001831e267164a1af46"><strong>顾客：</strong>参与项目过程实际体验的人</p>
<p id="b-4c9405f4129c4480bdb104a79420294f"><strong>技师&amp;服务人员：</strong></p>
<p id="b-67dd8dc50ce445b29e0ef14bfdb31322">①佩戴工牌/出现身份提示/<span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:15px">穿着与商家业务相符的统一制服、工作服</span></span>；</p>
<p id="b-59866c819d4f45c6988fc1f1a2129908">②正在进行<span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>服务过程</strong></span></p>
<details id="b-c8b75b528a004c00aa30b65b4e87ef8a">
<summary><span style="color:#FF4A47">服务过程（定义不泛化，同义内容可泛化，适用于定义②）</span></summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-cd2909e17604403291be3df1399f0f04">
<li id="b-c6c317849e774f6d85017e4c930e2cb6"><span style="color:#FF4A47">整理、摆放商家经营项目相关的设施、用品</span></li>
<li id="b-fa53aaa4414c474bae858b08a79559e7"><span style="color:#FF4A47">正在进行足疗、按摩等服务环节</span></li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-156e954800304422992d80e8c49d28fc">③为顾客提供项目体验的相关工作人员。</p>
</div>
</details>
<p id="b-b3126cc1d6ff416db964354bc6e66b4b"></p>
<p id="b-ad4c4ce2028e4635b38f46ea799e60ed"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-f0f3e6b9afa54a058830ba3cc9ffc12e">
<li id="b-27db518d395b4317b278cd53e63d9fa6"><span style="font-size:15px">正常卡通人物不打标，</span><span style="color:#000"><span style="font-size:15px">卡通人物如有明显的性感及暗示性行为等需打标。</span></span></li>
<li id="b-a1848bde4d2c45febe0384c1e7530fc3">店铺线下装饰、挂件（壁画、海报等）或<span style="color:#FF4A47">影视图片中出现的非真实女性形象。</span></li>
<li id="b-96ecd135e4b54ac99d672c1cdd5c0386">仅展示手部、脚部身体部位。</li>
<li id="b-88acb9bd4a5d448c86fba05e0349f964">新奇体验品类，商户名出现“猫咖、狗咖、鸟咖、爬宠、异宠、农场”等包含动物元素，且女性与动物同框或女性与动物互动。</li>
</ol>
</td>

<td id="b-b261db1283c74660a12868d15662c7f2" style="vertical-align:top">
<h1 id="b-211fa63c90a349f48e1706ab0cdbf0e6"><strong>内容低质</strong></h1>
</td>
<td id="b-18f18e012d4d433287eddfc84bf12be5" style="vertical-align:top">
<h2 id="b-3bdbe87cace84c1ea26c311b97a5b706">低信息量</h2>
</td>
<td id="b-f1baed49d1154249b5ae68839a5d91a4">
<p id="b-e299118595004df9a1fc2cec47599dfd">乱打</p>
</td>
<td id="b-ae47cbb090cb44c6ac17e01689d253d8">
<p id="b-3ccf6b922e9440a48f58efc231bcd2dc">乱打</p>
</td>
<td id="b-238028b68d9a4794b3c6c09f167511f2">
<p id="b-cb49ba25b4ad4bd7bce61fd53ebaf9ac"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>打标：</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-ea80aa5bf65e4e0f9dec69e58ed02c0c">
<li id="b-db2a800e0ca2437b9d295b5d04756851"><span style="font-size:15px">文本内容仅为</span><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:15px">大于3个的</span></span><span style="font-size:15px">纯字母/纯数字/纯标点符号/纯特殊符号，且无实际意义</span></li>
</ol>
<ol id="b-e3f07397daed4db0aca763873c1b40f0">
<li id="b-a22ad3744fee4624bab58a2d42d07155">文本内容为<span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:15px">大于3个的</span></span>上述四种形式（纯字母/纯数字/纯标点符号/纯特殊符号）的任意排列组合，且无实际意义</li>
</ol>
<ol id="b-68c112ce13a9484fbc8b0f09fa86f502">
<li id="b-85ac28d22b454ab09ebc48751bc41d72"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:15px">文本内容为</span></span>大于3个的纯表情符号打击</li>
</ol>
<ol id="b-6a860b5e9fcd4400a6776c16e68c6cb3">
<li id="b-f7f0fbd7cab945e3ab9c6b392d704fbe"><span style="font-size:15px">无任何体验，整句都是看不懂的、无意义的内容。</span></li>
</ol>
<ol id="b-9bfd0a158d2a4f42a1414790d55727fb">
<li id="b-03d5a8251b9745d2b80aed6c52d74b1f">乱打&gt;体验内容，也认为是乱打。</li>
</ol>
<p id="b-b6ddc4c81c444060b1827f6ccb21510e"></p>
<p id="b-a5066f3f94034720ac9d43b2487b488a"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:15px"><strong>需注意：</strong></span></span></p>
<ol id="b-f65669b4a94f4673bf1d2dce60a78300">
<li id="b-1281ba4d537546b8877b1d8c5c386f00"><span style="color:#000"><span style="font-size:15px">用户昵称不用这条规则，用户个人昵称可以用数字加字母组合。</span></span></li>
<li id="b-29e73315ebde4b028b09e60f5916cc2a">
<p id="b-ca913d6a22df414a94bcd5a0d189372a"><span style="color:#000"><span style="font-size:15px"><strong>颜文字</strong></span></span><span style="color:#000"><span style="font-size:15px">需整体看，无需单独查标点符号个数</span></span></p>
<details id="b-e9fe8c4110924d01afb1d6b6bea89ce5">

<p id="b-4d63adbc27404f7982603e13be3ed12f"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免：</strong></span></p>

<li id="b-63b5e645d3874b5f80a00b64076ada03"><span style="font-size:15px">外语&amp;方言，翻译后为有意义内容</span></li>

<li id="b-4d63823a1cca44e0a5c0a855159acafe">出现各种代码乱码的情况，【低信息量】相关标准豁免。</li>
</ol>
<ol id="b-9bf1be4854e640ffb6dd690cd76e963b">
<li id="b-d81ee260385d4be49a3da1d7e899ec6f"><span style="font-size:15px">以0-9开头的日期、时间</span></li>

<details id="b-0686168ac6904a5ea8440e2dc95c8faa">
<summary>日期示例：</summary>
<div>

</div>
</details>
<ol id="b-49282a2b10e1436a9a9b50160fbb52d7">
<li id="b-2ea1e5f13c9147758fdc707d230d3d3f">“啊”“哈”作为语气词，重复的“？”“！”“？！”“......”，出现在体验内容后，通过重复来加强语气时，不属于乱打内容</li>
<li id="b-27abb1f2f7f34a18b47444eda6ee8022">【交互类信息,问题/回答】类内容只进审回答部分，审核页面只有简短的答复文字，不属于无意义内容，可正常通过<span style="color:#FF4A47">。</span></li>
</ol>

<p id="b-7f4b0b23342d41d6899f21dca4b9b46c"><span style="font-size:15px">无任何体验，整句都是看不懂的、无意义的内容</span></p>

<p id="b-8af45cb92ffc4d4ba868d5697407def0"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-fb0b8b04221643ae8c0cd9e8f85aede9">
<p id="b-aba1010e4ecc4bf6b01483a7afdd1b42"></p>
<p id="b-1ffbe01cebb9482795455f0ad9ac55e2"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202631086182?contentType=1&amp;isNewContent=false">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>

<p id="b-03330c0cf4684534aba54c5630650a45"></p>
<ol id="b-83e876cc4ef54bdcb6bbe0201ffbb9d8">
<li id="b-22d2260d59af4e7aaa2da1db59259e95">

</li>
<li id="b-b4ac2ddc08174d38a2b0197a8b08f12e">
<p id="b-ddff2e517b534a92b1222679335ff173">相关话题+体验评价</p>
<details id="b-6cdfc9225b1141b7bb0845b1a0416362">
<summary>案例</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-25ca34ecc9464fcf877d35330a022147"><span style="font-size:15px">把把骚鸭心（30串）# #把把骚鸭心（30串）# #把把骚鸭心（30串）#</span><span style="font-size:15px"><strong>很好吃</strong></span></p>

</li>
<li id="b-42a6cf69f3f0422e8c6a4855fec7842c">
<p id="b-35acacb23b9045449980bec3e3ff08b3"><span style="font-size:15px">外语&amp;方言，翻译后为有意义内容</span></p>
<details id="b-b497224f5fcc4b3daebdb298426e6a2b">
<summary>案例</summary>
<div>
<ul id="b-df259bccf746475b956e7d993d34b20c">
<li id="b-5c5a834a1d464948844c8fe7ba5595a0"><span style="font-size:15px">点评内容：地址市二宫地铁站上边就係喇交通其实都几方便嘅但係呢间商场个卖场唔係好大架咋佢好细，而且佢裏边呢主要哩都係买啲海鲜啊同埋嗰啲其他嗰啲熟食呢佢而家都有啲但係唔係话好多蔬果吖水果之类但係又早瞓呢就好少啦同埋啲酸奶呢佢啲品种呢唔係咁，超级多，有啲可惜嘅就係要扮佢哋个会员呢係要畀钱先至有架，希望下次佢哋啲会员呢可以免费唔使畀钱就可以做到佢哋会员噉就好喇。</span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-cf9e8b8ec8cd44098ce2fefc2cc1f497"><span style="font-size:15px">喜欢的菜：香满楼酸奶纤维</span></p>
<hr id="b-648ddc12cabf4711afad45d1f8693743">
<ul id="b-ed8b27301602459e83271b1a9c7ff906">
<li id="b-ff011efa210346209b04df7cd13cdca1"><span style="font-size:15px">في المرة الأولى التي كنت تأكل هذا  ، قليلا مكلفة ولكن ضخمة لذيذالكورية التعليق قبل مثيرة جدا للاهتمام  ، تريد التعليق على اللعب فقط لم أفكر حتى لذيذ هههه</span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-25395a75d67a47118d7e8139056b94e9"><span style="font-size:15px">翻译后：我第一次吃这个，有点贵，但巨大的美味的朝鲜的评论之前，很有趣，你想玩的评论只是没有想到这么美味的哈哈</span></p>
<hr id="b-59e321d315dc46059d7d36a3e9aa57c5">
<ul id="b-b48af00c6f0647eea896e0b207d5c8d8">
<li id="b-3f16b19352ed47bba1c4e5d44e868ca7"><span style="font-size:15px">Ämnet som kommer naturligt, följer med dess naturliga trend, åtföljer lämpligt, naturligt kärlekstal naturligt.När du ser något som du gillar, tänker du på att köpa det åt dig och när du träffar något intressant, tänker du på att dela det med dig.Jag ringer när som helst när jag saknar dig, och när jag är tröt t säger jag att jag inte behöver stanna hos dig hela tiden.</span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-064c5a737b2c4594a20caf3b3d256eb9"><span style="font-size:15px">翻译后：当你看到你喜欢的东西，你会想买给自己，当你遇到有趣的事情，你会想和你分享。我想你的时候会给你打电话，当我累了，我说我不必一直和你在一起。</span></p>
<ul id="b-6463147fce4b42dea535436294f4d134">
<li id="b-990669a2f55646ed992ed6a3e11bf8bc">
<p id="b-3609af8a58f740ec933cbf295e6f6c86"><span style="font-size:15px">Have a fling with a friend</span></p>
<p id="b-10217f0a250249ddbaf90be9d2a84ccd"><span style="font-size:15px">翻译后：</span>与一个朋友有一段短暂恋情</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</details>
</li>
<li id="b-e93f324d3d414f76bba8af31aec83215">

</td>
<td id="b-9e8a6eba114b402881b94cb1e333323c">
<h2 id="b-87ef3fa368414b97ab16814223af1d81"><strong>商家侵权作弊</strong></h2>
</td>
<td id="b-904543e5ee594a299db09f65010fa4ce">
<p id="b-6c0c6c359fcb4bd7a2d43c7aa1e3de90"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-ab37887cd94343f3b4dcdeb05b8fce36">
<p id="b-04d8f32aa92d4af7b56dbee9cacf3321">图/视-商户乱传乱用“必吃榜”信息，诱导消费者认知误</p>
</td>
<td id="b-b785a0c7ff124db09123045abd01c19c">
<p id="b-e3504cd27f804117ae1c4f9a4581fe8a"><strong>【图/视/文】</strong></p>
<p id="b-6051fa8cf5544cb18e4ecc12d6302d1d">商户乱传乱用“必xx榜”信息，诱导消费者认知有误</p>
<p id="b-5a5acff3312742978affc701c91949d4"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-06193d6ce7ed4bbeb59f174b935d68d9">
<li id="b-74ac620c7b714f8fa9efefd4e4bead96">【图/视/文】中出现：必吃榜、<span style="color:#000">必x榜</span>等，本体或变体需打标，包含但不限于必吃餐厅、必住榜、必玩榜等。</li>
<li id="b-ad38ed02dc544b06be690a5f8f90bac6">
<p id="b-dfe100b18e3849139f6e1ae3286ab1eb">【图/视】中出现“必吃榜”各种图标【<strong>角标、对钩和配色</strong>】及变体形状见删。</p>
<details id="b-77f6916ab8cb405e97d8e4fb5e3c558e">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>角标举例（适用于定义2）</strong></span></summary>
<div>
<p id="b-004881a0b0424c20acc95098c11aab49"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236732051688?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236727308208?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
<p id="b-67cab96d3e3b4704b7d66ded95754d0e"><span style="color:#000">箭头+底色</span></p>
<p id="b-0c24374ab9f04689892cfa586a92dead"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236725676626?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236728106241?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/236727945050?&amp;isNewContent=false&amp;contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</div>

<p id="b-dc973d2e732e406eaf0c9cea4046ed4c"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-2680eb050fd04068bcc18a9f6f8ec8d6">
<li id="b-1d110a29b09342c4ad8c41256aedb249"><span style="color:#FF4A47">所有必吃榜、必X榜的标准线下场景，包括各类图标（角标、对钩和配色）、文字等</span></li>
</ol>
<p id="b-9de0ece000d94ee0abd92bdf201a2dcd"><span style="color:rgb(17, 17, 17)">执行type：</span></p>

<li id="b-ad53fb0145a9434ca3daa4c28231a347">40248，<span style="font-size:14px">40041，40190，40084，</span><span style="color: "><span style="font-size:14px">40042，</span></span><span style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.87)">252</span></li>
<li id="b-e87679f3f70343d5a6f148f0938e8c33">11546，10702，<span style="font-size:14px">11272，</span>10705，<span style="font-size:14px">10199，</span><span style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.87)">10103</span></li>
</ol>
<p id="b-530c082f6bdc44068b385b97270f3bcc"></p>
</td>

<ol id="b-67b6a9f4b92543869fa484e37f0d6ce8">
<li id="b-32a9d365160c4b0f869120f03683aed8">文字出现：必吃榜、必x榜等，本体或变体</li>
</ol>
<ul id="b-a889cee3c171453690bcebd2f70e8a5f">
<li id="b-d668de5677994522957dcafec9f514ef"><span style="font-size:15px">泰库辣♥浪漫双人、必♥吃♥榜♥泰浪漫双人</span></li>
<li id="b-d126d047b3fb4571a6a7efde21a7084f"><span style="font-size:15px">♥️必.吃.榜四人尊享套餐、餐后水果、水果</span></li>
<li id="b-ac18786745ef444f9780571891db105e"><span style="font-size:15px">双人打卡必吃榜套餐、扒类、比目鱼 蘑菇 苹果奶油汁</span></li>
<li id="b-2af7569f8d4b4d82a7ec39ebbb176144"><span style="font-size:15px">黔渔翁~贵州必 吃 榜！【市中心+西湖边+凤起路+嘉里中心】</span></li>
<li id="b-1891c39bf6664ae89a4b4a1c0b5872df"><span style="font-size:15px">聚划算，吃多少送多少 南昌大众必吃榜，好评榜的贰官进驻合肥啦！</span></li>
<li id="b-b2c982e03b994109ab707cb4fd65c825"><span style="font-size:15px">南城香必吃榜</span></li>
</ul>

<td id="b-45c5e2387f154e5aafc17033a84a5701">
<h2 id="b-8e94ccc5c3ac41fe9e1371b1c90e13e4"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>商家侵权作弊（严控）</strong></span></h2>
</td>
<td id="b-d552cf9eac7d417ea98c9ac2c3c56dc3">
<p id="b-b98cb06d8a5d40d883afca909eb0d2a1"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-59d9bbf8ca78455abd42e7aa1ab3a3ef">
<p id="b-110dfc73060549b2958251a152266e05">图/视-商户乱传乱用“必吃榜”信息，诱导消费者认知误</p>
</td>
<td id="b-53b0ebe0260f474fa1a401b91d9d4965">
<p id="b-e770e83ecd4a4e9c94c5b60ebe9acf06"><strong>【图/视/文】</strong></p>
<p id="b-56e4d75a2fc144b6b872f040438f63ec">商户乱传乱用“必xx榜”信息，诱导消费者认知有误</p>
<p id="b-dad6b8a025fe4c609cb78084d7a4741f"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>打标</strong></span></p>
<ol id="b-8d62dd1a3fb34f24bc48e17ca04aa6c6">
<li id="b-1f95b748d7d640398ab36636a41aba81">【图/视/文】中出现：必吃榜、<span style="color:#000">必吃、必玩、必住、必x榜</span>等，本体或变体需打标，包含但不限于必吃餐厅、必住榜、必玩榜等。</li>
<li id="b-b923a4c19d7f4a30ab4969f5480f620c">
<p id="b-135802a191be445b823e9888bdc801eb">【图/视】中出现“必吃榜”各种图标【<strong>角标、对钩和配色</strong>】及变体形状见删。</p>
<details id="b-356277f3048e444f9c29388d2606ddd6">
<summary><span style="color:#000"><strong>角标举例（适用于定义2）</strong></span></summary>

</td>
<td id="b-a359603ecbe0430ebb02b796bf396892">

</td>
<td id="b-155eab9544474ae1bdc0f8de010f08eb">

</td>
<td id="b-7b7b870973674b30b2e161ba33ef3597">
<p id="b-331a85000f6b4d1c8acbaabbc2bd0e6b"></p>
<p id="b-ff4abc58a5be41f390367ca9505555cc"><span style="color:#000"><strong>普通话范畴：</strong></span><span style="color:#000">仅普通话、北京话、河北话、东北话。</span><br><span style="color:#000"><strong>​非普通话范畴：</strong></span><span style="color:#000">除上述外其他方言、少数民族语言、外文。</span><br><span style="color:#000">​</span></p>
<p id="b-b3413e42f3e74ebca49d725c7f0f842c"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>打标</strong></span><br><span style="color:#000">​1.非普通话见删。</span><br><span style="color:#000">​2.普通话无违规，正常通过；若违规，按照听懂后的内容打标对应违规点标签。</span></p>

<p id="b-e4a55dba9b2142d296a881705fa9aa24">1. 对象：🍵、茶、鸡、蛋蛋后、楼凤、站街、出来卖、卖肉、卖奶、<span style="color:#000">卖屁股、嫩</span>模、空乘、空姐、公关、<span style="color:#D41E21">年龄限制+老师（参考新增案例）</span></p>
<p id="b-7b5afde3e16745c3831f696bb51cf0f1">2. 项目：附近好车、验车报告、赶场不、饮料到位、修车全套、极品探花、上门、宅急、宅家、宅约、酒店按摩、酒店SPA、酒店预约、酒店式按摩、的士带客到酒店中奖、独特服务、选妃、海选、前列腺保养、弟弟解压、弟弟宝藏地（弟弟指向称呼男性下体）、擦边球、丝足、制服诱惑、飘飘欲仙、年轻职员多才多艺、该有的都有、懂得都懂、配合度高、男性专属服务、男人的天堂、男士专属、男士基地、快乐男士、陪酒、陪唱、陪聊、挂毛巾、蓝裤子、果冻。</p>
<p id="b-e196d706c466454b9f463986c6e5d319"><span style="color:#000">3.涉及汽油类、飞机类、荤素类询问或回答</span></p>

<p id="b-c3cee79fd1d74bffa8bba45523b0146b"><span style="font-size:14px">租车妹子陪玩团单5888一天。</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-514ac8323a2d450da0057fe56789297b">
<p id="b-b933b1f303be458aa05c5c5cef44546b"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202631953974?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202630610518?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202629697318?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202631396553?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202630214277?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202632271031?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
</td>
<td id="b-8038e08009464aa3a463e04e103fa1ea">
<p id="b-6d9cb4b2d3c64976a1398bf0dae0ff29">辖区张某误入大学生兼职擦边陪聊被骗2000元，注意不要上当 - 东湖派出所宣。</p>
<p id="b-6e6bf5ea8d3647c0ab06ecf8cf083440"></p>
<p id="b-bfb8fd6477354144997ee63deaac5f4e">广大市民朋友们提高自身抵抗诱惑能力，保持健康的上网习惯，所谓“艳遇”不过是犯罪分子设下的圈套，遇到陌生网友主动添加好友进行视频裸聊时，不要相信所谓“激情”说辞，不要下载来路不明的APP，更不要向陌生人泄露身份和家庭等敏感信息，一旦发现被敲诈勒索后，不要选择沉默，保存好聊天记录、银行账号等信息，第一时间报警，避免遭受更大经济损失。</p>
<p id="b-d7daa9857c7c4a81b525dddd21da3a62"></p>
<p id="b-27e65adc99904a1abb6a5599e2b65923"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/202629539042?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
<p id="b-525906663e964f77ae71b2222725686d"></p>
</td>

<tr id="b-31e4527d3b4b4218ae7fd9e8abac7f0e">
<td rowspan="3" id="b-4a06a6b43f3746918839009e54704524">
<p id="b-26620e6d2bae4645b44123c231b09f96" style="text-align:start">性挑逗</p>
</td>
<td id="b-445e523e00984fc1aad10e97d0d80e75">
<p id="b-c539d20aefe0429fb36d9158bc30a5f4">穿着暴露</p>
<p id="b-6f272eec9cb04fe9a461ba4e4f1b379e">穿着暴露未完全遮蔽敏感部位</p>
</td>
<td id="b-0fc46cd2d0f94ab2a022e0180bad76f6">
<p id="b-091262a34e8948299cd957ea5e7cdf26"><span style="color:#FF4A47">打标：</span></p>
<p id="b-602c4ae9c1754f719ae5115b2c655b0f">穿着暴露</p>
<p id="b-62b449a5a4cc401bbdbaaa38f7c309f0">1）女性上半身穿着</p>
<ul id="b-ea0abb1f6f9a4750bf050f002f3a2c24">
<li id="b-2f1094feea334abf9be876264222bbc5">正面/侧面裸露乳房下缘，侧缘</li>
</ul>
<ul id="b-21b6077f21d847d1abb5e472db01f713">
<li id="b-21bd0de043d24b50b5e23f700fded510">背面裸露并露乳房侧缘或者臀沟</li>
</ul>
<p id="b-2afd93bdcc754dc381c6006766b4abb3">2）「下半身穿着暴露」（不分男女）</p>
<ul id="b-ccc0e30ff200401ca70314c88fef722d">
<li id="b-1c1f7908f1e24879806b0878995a7578">穿着开档裤/开档丝袜</li>
<li id="b-f0d1cf01435f46e48115a709a6123dc3"><span style="color: "><span style="font-size:14px">下身内裤正面小于敏感三角区或者背面露臀沟</span></span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-ff04f4ef0f694349b5844302d25975d3"></p>
<p id="b-e391fb4d075a4468ad953e0d9e3703c7"><span style="color: "><span style="font-size:14px">3）敏感部位</span></span></p>
<p id="b-b2529ec2f8c64b848665fd9fe1956c36"><span style="color: "><span style="font-size:14px">①未穿衣物拿肢体或其他物品，光影效果遮挡</span></span><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:14px">敏感部位</span></span></p>
<p id="b-44ca9884191347188764bd7d4c597015"><span style="color: "><span style="font-size:14px">② 裸体但没有露出</span></span><span style="color:#FF4A47"><span style="font-size:14px">敏感部位</span></span></p>
<p id="b-5fa2ad8c614b4010a4c103f8092f72ca"></p>
<p id="b-340e6d939de34149838eb1af0142616b"><span style="color:#00BA73"><strong>豁免：</strong></span>乳房侧缘或者臀沟，仅遮挡私密部位画面在以下场景可通过</p>
<p id="b-67b6a0192fcb452baa40f42284f79a0f">场景：医疗、丽人、摄影作品、<span style="color:rgb(50, 50, 50)"><span style="font-size:14px">亲子、按摩场景下，</span></span></p>
<p id="b-9ca2d05e32dc479585e24fa95d9a54ad"></p>
</td>
<td id="b-4ebc613dbff6482d8fa9a8e7024812ea">

<p id="b-ede7265d97a844dd8e32946a71518057"></p>
</td>

<tr id="b-f0272c9cd4814e0a8c1e6dca20701c64">
<td id="b-2621694956a84a278ad7d260e6f7dd38">
<p id="b-27a7c49be6a046ea92caa972967a09df">凸显敏感部位</p>
<p id="b-a1ec5e5c35e84600a70252f2354acea2">使用性挑逗动作，通过聚焦等方式凸显敏感部位</p>
</td>
<td id="b-88a8c393e8304117ab59109b5611b8cf">
<p id="b-567dc7c6c4b045838b18932d0a6f6d25"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><strong>打标：</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-40005308801742e08ab690eae73f86f6">凸显敏感部位方式</p>
<p id="b-955d7753c963437e89d193fb421d5f53">凸显胸部</p>
<ul id="b-70b4b1b26de3487d86944878557bce24">
<li id="b-45e22f9a22444d55bc0411c9b0c4a672">揉胸、抖胸、挤乳房动作</li>
<li id="b-f179c99948b047459c435c7fd6f8287a">乳房上，乳沟间放置物品（贴纸，项链等装饰品不打标）</li>
<li id="b-b8b365b4ff254481938a9d598fef5222"><span style="font-size:15px">通过掀起、敞开、拉下、绷紧、弄湿、撕破内/外衣出现乳沟、</span></li>
</ul>
<p id="b-cf59421518dd43dd8e46afc168c15040">凸显下半身</p>
<ul id="b-552cfdadf2184ebea91950b3317e5578">
<li id="b-59f30344d9d4430683c02fff0c90c9f8">撅臀、抖臀（视频主体，图片不打）</li>
<li id="b-7e8fb89e56f0462c8931214135f4ce5d">
<p id="b-426032d9dc644267aed5f10774eaaf43"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>大风险定义：主镜视角聚焦腿部（人物未全身出镜，裸露至膝盖以上的大腿）</strong></span></p>
<p id="b-b23ee8f01d0f4a0ab21b16e5796975b4"><span class="image-unavailable" title="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/215278412516?contentType=1">【图片未选入本次 500 张同步】</span></p>
<p id="b-49b1c5cd27284fa69fabd9fadcf7d112"><span style="color:#D41E21"><strong>小风险定义：聚焦/特写腿部，以展示腿部为目的（大腿），结合语境、环境氛围有色情暗示意图和导向</strong></span></p>
<details id="b-ac78940b38f649f3b66a196978e30569">
<summary>风险定义解释</summary>
<div>
<p id="b-1f562c5a44a7480f985499c1feb1a0d8"><span style="color:#FF4A47"><em><strong>风险定义即为标准定义，常见场景仅帮助同学去理解定义，执行思路提供统一思路给同学判断，优先按照风险定义进行审核，如遇过于难判断问题再参考辅助判断</strong></em></span></p>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-043864379c0c41de96e08e23b0b85bd0">
<summary>管控背景+管控原则</summary>
<div>
<blockquote id="b-5a8543c36cd9475994292a8cc97fdf0b">
<p id="b-d1e8a6d7318642d08e09f582636fb3bc"><strong>1.管控背景</strong></p>
<p id="b-b251ac53906748b89b7a56b2f47ab436"><strong>《互联网信息服务管理办法》</strong>：第十五条规定</p>
<p id="b-9fd6a43f7a0442d6b40181d6e165a13c">互联网信息服务提供者不得制作、复制、发布、传播含有 “散布淫秽、色情、赌博、暴力、凶杀、恐怖或者教唆犯罪的” 以及 “侮辱或者诽谤他人，侵害他人合法权益的” 内容。如果聚焦腿部特写的内容涉及色情、侮辱或诽谤他人等情形，则违反该办法。</p>
<p id="b-fdd127e1d71c4f8b99d1258a883f94a4"><strong>《刑法》：</strong>第 363 条、第 364 条规定</p>
<p id="b-ae7bd1944e964991880e9949bb9f56cc">制作、传播淫秽物品罪是指明知是淫秽物品而予以制作、复制、出版、贩卖、运输或者通过信息网络向公众传播的行为，如果聚焦腿部特写的视频内容具有色情性质，行为人可能构成制作、传播淫秽物品罪。<br><strong>《未成年人网络保护条例》</strong>：第二十二条</p>
<p id="b-d0c319d1e9294fa7a8372aa80785ee93">明令禁止制作、发布含有宣扬淫秽、色情等内容的网络信息。对于涉及未成年人的腿部特写内容，如果存在性暗示或不符合未成年人保护原则的情况，将受到该条例的约束。</p>
<p id="b-f47dffd2763249c3804bc0b27613c947"><strong>《平台相关规定》</strong></p>
<p id="b-e44573762a194221822832347ced939e">各网络平台也有针对聚焦腿部特写等不当内容的规定。例如抖音规定，短视频或直播严禁画面拍摄突出展示敏感部位或镜头一直聚焦敏感部位，如从上往下仰拍臀部与腿部等，对违反规定的将根据严重程度进行处罚，包括视频不推荐、限制或禁止投稿、账号降级、永久禁言等。斗鱼平台规定，主播拍摄角度不得由上至下拍摄胸部等敏感部位，由下至上拍摄腿部、臀部等敏感部位，不得长时间聚焦腿部、脚部等敏感部位。</p>
<p id="b-932a929dea1a47ebbc6833fa52bbc851"><strong>《网络信息内容生态治理规定》</strong>：第七条规定</p>
<p id="b-2a5a9c9a40644fa2a1d3ce82b7c8182f">网络信息内容生产者应当采取措施，防范和抵制制作、复制、发布含有 “带有性暗示、性挑逗等易使人产生性联想的” 以及 “宣扬低俗、庸俗、媚俗内容的” 不良信息。低俗性感聚焦腿拍摄内容通常会涉及性暗示、性挑逗，且属于宣扬低俗内容，违反了该规定。</p>
<p id="b-f73aed5c234a4e5589922d4351949f48"><strong>2.管控原则</strong></p>
<p id="b-46272ffb0f1b48b487607cac78273089">1）绝对大尺度的色情暴露</p>
<p id="b-051ec3d843224a80a530690c1006bcb7">2）低俗的性暗示导向，这部分模糊空间难以用法律严格定义和区别，因此风险取决于用户感受-&gt;举报次数/舆论体量-&gt;监管关注，因此个人视觉感受是非常重要的一部分</p>
<p id="b-91f7fe4517ee45ff8292c773e7601680">聚焦腿部位通过镜头构图、姿势、或服饰搭配（聚焦丝袜、短裤与腿部的暴露区域）制造性暗示氛围。</p>
</blockquote>
</div>
</details>
<details id="b-7cb74ae256ac4dc3a0c53b8845dacfa5">
<summary>常见场景（优先按照风险定义执行）+执行思路</summary>
<div>
<table id="b-1eb41f07dcfd4e05b7498aa53c693f15">
<tr id="b-1b91b3a258414a46a87fe250f66551c5">
<th id="b-82e0fd9f2fd340f18f4e08b65a548f76">
<p id="b-4a264bd49ccf40f9964aae02d931a8b6" style="text-align:center"><strong>常见场景(第一眼就看见是腿)</strong></p>
</th>
<th id="b-5e0ca406cfc34fb1bba87bb3377b7f8a">
<p id="b-ea542c6dfb51477a9e7e76c3b79fc8e1" style="text-align:center"><strong>执行思路</strong></p>
</th>
</tr>
<tr id="b-1bef4cde63fe44678d14e2e6d9cb80f8">
<td id="b-fbabe135aa3e4c638e6f01b248058e46" style="vertical-align:middle">
<p id="b-a08d8c27691b40ca84628537e8215b56">隐私空间（室内）：沙发、床、浴缸、卫生间、阴暗空间等地方，凸显腿部。</p>

<td rowspan="4" id="b-8a6cfad7d7f54fd7b662eab75447bb26">
<p id="b-3186604930784dda9d64899b358be854"><strong>执行从严</strong></p>
<figure src="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/215276496626?contentType=0" width="1611" height="221" id="b-e95b7d9e135243df94eb1328d2b65171"><figcaption>思维导图：思维导图（原始链接，可能需要学城权限）</figcaption><a href="https://km.sankuai.com/api/file/cdn/2732771854/215276496626?contentType=0">在原文服务中查看</a></figure>

<td id="b-bef369027efb45228ae4f494125b473d" style="vertical-align:middle">
<p id="b-377dfaf401824241b8c039d7484ab385"></p>
<p id="b-141c9db9199645cf9df26113ff8041c9">不良姿势：刻意通过腿部交叉、跪姿、翘腿、M腿、青蛙趴等动作营造性暗示氛围，凸显腿部。</p>
<p id="b-b0dcd86382b04522b4bc7b4ef5ff1fad"></p>
</td>
</tr>

<p id="b-ccb2cb029c984041b1817fc8d404d762">通过穿着：短裤、短裙、丝袜、特殊制服等凸显腿部。</p>
